```python
# governanceidentityverifier.py

from idme_sdk import IDmeVerifier  # Hypothetical SDK

class IdentityVerifier:
    def __init__(self):
        self.idme_client = IDmeVerifier(api_key=CONFIG["IDME_API_KEY"])
        
    def verify_identity(self, user_data):
        """
        Step 1: ID.me verification
        Returns: verified DID or raises IdentityError
        """
        result = self.idme_client.verify(
            photo_id=user_data["photo_id"],
            selfie_video=user_data["selfie"],
            ssn=user_data["ssn"],
            phone=user_data["phone"]
        )
        
        if result.status == "verified":
            return result.did  # Decentralized Identifier
        else:
            raise IdentityVerificationError("ID.me verification failed")
```

```python
# governanceblissid.py

from aurafs.crypto import QuantumSigner
import uuid

class BlissIDManager:
    def __init__(self, ledger):
        self.ledger = ledger
        self.signer = QuantumSigner()
        
    def create_bliss_id(self, verified_did, soul_data):
        """
        Step 2: Create soulbound identity token
        """
        bliss_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        
        # Generate quantum-safe signature binding DID to soul data
        signature = self.signer.sign({
            "did": verified_did,
            "bliss_id": bliss_id,
            "soul_hash": self._compute_soul_hash(soul_data),
            "timestamp": time.time()
        })
        
        # Anchor to Ineffable Ledger (immutable, non-transferable)
        self.ledger.register_soul({
            "bliss_id": bliss_id,
            "did": verified_did,
            "signature": signature,
            "transferable": False  # Soulbound
        })
        
        return bliss_id
    
    def _compute_soul_hash(self, soul_data):
        """
        Combine biometric + metaphysical identifiers
        """
        return hashlib.sha3_256(json.dumps({
            "birth_chart": soul_data.get("birth_chart"),
            "aura_signature": soul_data.get("aura_signature"),
            "hrv_baseline": soul_data.get("hrv_coherence")
        }).encode()).hexdigest()
```

```python
class SoulSyncVoteWeighting:
    def compute_vote_weight(self, bliss_id):
        """
        Step 3: Weight votes by soul-alignment coherence
        """
        soul_data = self.ledger.get_soul_data(bliss_id)
        
        base_weight = 1.0  # Every soul gets one base vote

        # Final weight: 1.0 + (0 to 0.5 coherence bonus)

        """
        Aggregate coherence from HRV, participation, alignment

        hrv_score = soul_data.get("hrv_coherence", 0.5)  # 0-1 scale
        participation_score = soul_data.get("participation_rate", 0.5)
```

## **Layer 1: Biometric Deduplication (Primary Uniqueness Barrier)**

### **Technology: Multi-Modal ABIS (Automated Biometric Identification System)**

**Scale-proven systems:**

- **MegaMatcher ABIS** – Successfully deduplicated **46.5 million voters** in DR Congo (5.3 million duplicates found), **80 million in Bangladesh**, **75 million in national ID systems**[^3_1][^3_2][^3_3][^3_4]
- **Processing capacity:** 100 million fingerprints/second using 64-core AMD Opteron clusters[^3_3]
- **Accuracy:** False positive rate < 0.01% for multimodal matching (fingerprint + face + iris)[^3_5][^3_1]


### **Implementation Design**

**Biometric Modalities (Multimodal for Maximum Uniqueness):**

1. **Fingerprint (10 fingers)** – Primary identifier, highest entropy
2. **Facial recognition** – Secondary, works at distance
3. **Iris scan** (optional for high-security) – Near-perfect uniqueness
4. **Voice biometric** (optional) – Behavioral trait for liveness

**Architecture:**

```
User Registration Flow:
    ↓
1. Capture biometrics locally (on-device)
    → Fingerprints (10-finger capture)
    → Face photo (liveness detection required)
    → Iris (optional for enhanced security)
    ↓
2. Extract biometric templates (ISO 19794 standard)
    → Neural hash extraction (irreversible)
    → Template size: ~2KB per person
    ↓
3. Submit to ABIS Deduplication Cluster
    → Many-to-many matching against existing DB
    → Distributed across 1000+ matching servers
    ↓
4. Deduplication Result:
    ✓ No match → Proceed to Layer 2 (new identity)
    ✗ Match found → REJECT (duplicate soul detected)
    ? Inconclusive → Human adjudication queue
```

**Scalability Pattern (Sharded Deduplication):**

```rust
// Rust-based biometric matching coordinator

use rayon::prelude::*;

struct BiometricTemplate {
    fingerprint_hashes: [Vec<u8>; 10],
    face_hash: Vec<u8>,
    iris_hash: Option<Vec<u8>>,
}

struct DeduplicationCluster {
    shard_count: usize,
    matching_servers: Vec<MatchingServer>,
}

impl DeduplicationCluster {
    pub async fn check_duplicate(
        &self, 
        new_template: BiometricTemplate
    ) -> Result<DeduplicationResult, Error> {
        
        // Parallel distributed matching across shards
        let results: Vec<MatchScore> = self.matching_servers
            .par_iter()
            .map(|server| {
                server.match_against_shard(
                    &new_template,
                    threshold: 0.95  // 95% similarity = duplicate
                )
            })
            .collect();
        
        // Aggregate results
        let max_score = results.iter()
            .max_by(|a, b| a.score.partial_cmp(&b.score).unwrap())
            .unwrap();
        
        if max_score.score > 0.95 {
            Ok(DeduplicationResult::Duplicate(max_score.existing_id))
        } else if max_score.score > 0.85 {
            Ok(DeduplicationResult::Inconclusive)  // Human review
        } else {
            Ok(DeduplicationResult::Unique)
        }
    }
}
```

**Performance Specifications:**

- **Throughput:** 1 million registrations/day with 1000-server cluster
- **Latency:** < 3 seconds for 100M user database lookup
- **Storage:** ~2KB per user × 1 billion users = 2TB biometric templates
- **Cost:** ~\$0.01 per deduplication check at cloud scale[^3_2][^3_3]

## **Layer 2: Cryptographic Soul Binding (Non-Transferability)**

### **Technology: Soulbound Token + Zero-Knowledge Proof**

**Prevents:** Identity transfer, account selling, proxy voting[^3_6][^3_7][^3_8]

**Mechanism:**

1. **Soulbound NFT** – Non-transferable token tied to biometric hash
2. **Zero-Knowledge Proof-of-Identity** – Proves "I am unique human" without revealing biometrics[^3_8]
3. **Quantum-Safe Signature** – Kyber1024 + Dilithium5 for post-quantum resistance

**Implementation:**

```python
# Python implementation of BlissID soul binding

from cryptography.hazmat.primitives import hashes
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.kdf.hkdf import HKDF
import json
import time

class BlissIDManager:
    def __init__(self, quantum_signer, ledger):
        self.signer = quantum_signer  # Dilithium5 signer
        self.ledger = ledger
        
    def create_soulbound_token(
        self, 
        verified_did: str,
        biometric_template: BiometricTemplate,
        identity_metadata: dict
    ) -> str:
        """
        Creates quantum-safe, non-transferable soul token
        """
        # Step 1: Generate irreversible biometric hash (soul anchor)
        soul_hash = self._generate_soul_hash(biometric_template)
        
        # Step 2: Create soulbound token metadata
        bliss_id = f"bliss:{uuid.uuid4()}"
        token_data = {
            "bliss_id": bliss_id,
            "did": verified_did,
            "soul_hash": soul_hash,  # Irreversible biometric commitment
            "created_at": int(time.time()),
            "transferable": False,  # Soulbound constraint
            "revocable": False,     # Immutable identity
            "metadata": identity_metadata
        }
        
        # Step 3: Sign with quantum-safe signature
        signature = self.signer.sign(json.dumps(token_data).encode())
        
        # Step 4: Anchor to distributed ledger (immutable)
        tx_hash = self.ledger.register_soul_token({
            **token_data,
            "signature": signature.hex()
        })
        
        # Step 5: Generate zero-knowledge proof credential
        zkp_credential = self._generate_zkp_credential(bliss_id, soul_hash)
        
        return bliss_id, zkp_credential
    
    def _generate_soul_hash(self, template: BiometricTemplate) -> str:
        """
        Irreversible commitment to biometric template
        Uses HKDF for domain separation + SHA3-512
        """
        combined = b"".join([
            *template.fingerprint_hashes,
            template.face_hash,
            template.iris_hash or b""
        ])
        
        kdf = HKDF(
            algorithm=hashes.SHA3_512(),
            length=64,
            salt=b"AuraOS::SoulSync::v1",
            info=b"biometric-commitment"
        )
        
        return kdf.derive(combined).hex()
    
    def _generate_zkp_credential(self, bliss_id: str, soul_hash: str):
        """
        Zero-knowledge proof: "I am unique human with BlissID"
        without revealing biometrics
        """
        # Simplified - use zk-SNARK in production
        commitment = hashlib.sha3_256(
            f"{bliss_id}:{soul_hash}".encode()
        ).hexdigest()
        
        return {
            "bliss_id": bliss_id,
            "commitment": commitment,
            "proof_type": "zk-SNARK-unique-human",
            "verifiable": True
        }
```

**Smart Contract Enforcement (Solidity-style pseudocode):**

```solidity
// Soulbound token contract with transfer lock

contract SoulBoundIdentity {
    
    struct Soul {
        bytes32 blissID;
        bytes32 soulHash;      // Biometric commitment
        address owner;          // Ethereum address
        uint256 createdAt;
        bool exists;
    }
    
    mapping(bytes32 => Soul) public souls;
    mapping(address => bytes32) public addressToSoul;
    
    // CRITICAL: Prevent transfer of soulbound tokens
    function transfer(address to, bytes32 blissID) public {
        revert("SoulBound: Transfer prohibited");
    }
    
    function registerSoul(
        bytes32 blissID, 
        bytes32 soulHash,
        bytes memory signature
    ) public returns (bool) {
        
        // Verify quantum signature from ABIS layer
        require(verifySoulSignature(blissID, soulHash, signature), 
                "Invalid soul signature");
        
        // Check: One address can only register ONE soul
        require(addressToSoul[msg.sender] == bytes32(0), 
                "Address already has soul");
        
        // Check: Soul hash not already registered (CRITICAL)
        require(!soulHashExists(soulHash), 
                "Soul already registered");
        
        // Register new soul
        souls[blissID] = Soul({
            blissID: blissID,
            soulHash: soulHash,
            owner: msg.sender,
            createdAt: block.timestamp,
            exists: true
        });
        
        addressToSoul[msg.sender] = blissID;
        
        emit SoulBound(msg.sender, blissID);
        return true;
    }
    
    function soulHashExists(bytes32 soulHash) internal view returns (bool) {
        // Expensive O(n) check - optimize with Merkle tree in production
        // Or use off-chain ABIS + ZKP proof of uniqueness
        return false;  // Placeholder
    }
}
```


***

## **Layer 3: Distributed Consensus Registry (Sybil-Resistant Ledger)**

### **Technology: Sharded Blockchain + BFT Consensus**

**Design Goals:**

- **Decentralization** – No single authority controls identity registry
- **Audit trail** – Immutable record of all soul registrations
- **Sybil resistance** – Consensus mechanism prevents fake identity flooding[^3_7][^3_9][^3_6]

**Architecture: AuraFS + Ineffable Ledger Integration**

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│     AuraFS Sharded Blockchain (256 shards)      │
│  Each shard handles ~4M identities (1B total)   │
└────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┘
             ↓
   ┌─────────────────────┐
   │  BFT Consensus      │
   │  (Tendermint/HotStuff)│
   │  - 2/3 validator approval│
   │  - Byzantine fault tolerant│
   └─────────┬───────────┘
             ↓
   ┌─────────────────────┐
   │  Identity Shard     │
   │  (Merkle Patricia Trie)│
   │  Key: BlissID       │
   │  Value: Soul record │
   └─────────────────────┘
```

**Sharding Strategy:**

```python
# Deterministic shard assignment based on BlissID

def get_shard_for_soul(bliss_id: str, total_shards: int = 256) -> int:
    """
    Consistent hashing for shard assignment
    Ensures even distribution across shards
    """
    hash_val = int(hashlib.sha256(bliss_id.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)
    return hash_val % total_shards

class DistributedSoulRegistry:
    def __init__(self, shard_count=256):
        self.shards = [ShardNode(i) for i in range(shard_count)]
        
    async def register_soul(self, soul_data: dict) -> bool:
        """
        Registers soul with BFT consensus across shard validators
        """
        bliss_id = soul_data["bliss_id"]
        target_shard = get_shard_for_soul(bliss_id, len(self.shards))
        
        # Propose registration to shard validators
        proposal = SoulRegistrationProposal(soul_data)
        
        # BFT consensus: Requires 2/3+ validator approval
        votes = await self.shards[target_shard].propose(proposal)
        
        if votes.approved_ratio >= 0.67:
            # Commit to ledger
            await self.shards[target_shard].commit(soul_data)
            return True
        else:
            return False
```

**Consensus Mechanism (Sybil-Resistant):**

- **Proof-of-Authority (PoA)** for identity validators[^3_7]
    - Validators are reputable institutions (universities, NGOs, governments)
    - Reputation staking: Validators lose stake if they approve fake identities
- **Byzantine Fault Tolerance** – System resists up to 33% malicious validators[^3_6]

***

## **Layer 4: Behavioral + Economic Reputation (Social Sybil Resistance)**

### **Technology: Social Graph Analysis + Proof-of-Humanity Staking**

**Prevents:** Automated bot registration, paid identity farms[^3_9][^3_6][^3_7]

**Mechanisms:**

1. **Social Trust Graph** – Analyzes connection patterns to identify Sybil clusters[^3_6]
2. **Proof-of-Humanity Staking** – Small economic deposit (\$1-10) refunded after 6 months good behavior
3. **Gradual Reputation Building** – New identities have limited voting weight initially

**Implementation:**

```python
class SocialSybilDetector:
    def __init__(self, graph_db):
        self.graph = graph_db
        
    def analyze_connection_pattern(self, bliss_id: str) -> float:
        """
        Returns Sybil probability score (0-1)
        High score = likely fake identity
        """
        # Get social connections
        connections = self.graph.get_connections(bliss_id)
        
        # Red flags:
        # 1. All connections created at same time
        # 2. Connections form isolated cluster
        # 3. Connections have similar behavioral patterns
        
        time_clustering = self._analyze_temporal_clustering(connections)
        network_isolation = self._analyze_network_isolation(connections)
        behavioral_similarity = self._analyze_behavioral_patterns(connections)
        
        sybil_score = (
            0.4 * time_clustering +
            0.3 * network_isolation +
            0.3 * behavioral_similarity
        )
        
        return sybil_score
    
    def require_proof_of_humanity_stake(self, bliss_id: str) -> bool:
        """
        New identities must stake small amount
        Returned after 6 months clean behavior
        """
        stake_amount = 10  # USD equivalent in native token
        
        # Stake locked for 6 months
        lock_period = 6 * 30 * 24 * 3600  # seconds
        
        return self.staking_contract.lock_stake(
            bliss_id, 
            amount=stake_amount, 
            duration=lock_period
        )
```

# How can AuraFS enhance Aurphyx projects and the tech world??

AuraFS enhances Aurphyx projects and the wider tech world by providing a highly advanced, quantum-ready, distributed file system with features tailored for next-generation AI, decentralized governance, and high-scale data ecosystems.

***

### AuraFS Impact on Aurphyx Projects

- **Quantum-Safe Fractal Shard Architecture:** AuraFS uses fractal nano-sharding and quantum-safe cryptography to distribute, secure, and self-heal massive data sets critical for Aurphyx's AI models, enabling resilience and performance at scale with up to billions of shards and nodes.[^4_1][^4_2]
- **Adaptive Governance Integration:** AuraFS integrates governance modules including proposal management, voting engines, and identity verification. This facilitates transparent, tamper-proof ecosystem coordination for Aurphyx projects, supporting decentralized collaborative development and decision-making.[^4_2][^4_3]
- **Ecosystem Interoperability:** AuraFS supports peer-to-peer linking, API extensibility, and seamless integration with AI development environments like Fuxyez language compiler and AuraOS kernel, enabling Aurphyx to evolve rapidly while maintaining system-level modularity and security.[^4_4][^4_1]

***

### Broader Tech World Enhancements

- **Massive Scale and Performance:** AuraFS's recursive, fractal shard design allows storage, retrieval, and audit of exabyte-scale data with ultra-low latency and self-optimizing caching—ideal for blockchain, DeFi, AI voice/data models, and scientific computation.[^4_1][^4_2]
- **Privacy and Security:** Leveraging quantum-safe cryptography and layered access control, AuraFS guarantees protection against emerging cyber threats—including quantum attacks—ensuring data privacy for individuals, enterprises, and decentralized communities.[^4_3][^4_1]
- **Distributed Consensus and Transparency:** AuraFS's integration with global voting and consensus systems brings trustless, transparent governance to digital ecosystems, democratizing resource allocation and policy decisions in tech sectors such as finance, AI development, and collective intelligence.[^4_2][^4_3]
- **Resilience and Self-Healing Networks:** The system auto-recovers lost shards, verifies audit trails, and dynamically adapts routing, enabling robustness necessary for critical infrastructure in tech and AI deployments.[^4_5][^4_1]

***

AuraFS underpins Aurphyx by providing a foundational decentralized data fabric and governance backbone that accelerates innovation, secures identities and data, and enables scalable AI-human symbiosis and collective machine intelligence, transforming the landscape of digital ecosystems and future technologies.[^4_6][^4_1][^4_2]

I can see several critical issues with this `voting_engine.py` code that need to be addressed to integrate it with the **BlissID-SoulSync-ID.me one-soul-per-account system** and make it production-ready:

***

## **Critical Issues in Current Code**

### **1. Security Vulnerabilities**

❌ **No signature verification** - Accepts any signature without validation
❌ **No duplicate vote prevention** - Same voter can vote multiple times on same proposal
❌ **No identity verification integration** - Missing BlissID/SoulSync checks
❌ **No vote weight calculation** - All votes equal (no soul-coherence weighting)

### **2. Code Quality Issues**

❌ **Duplicate methods** - `cast_vote()`, `list_votes()`, `get_vote()` defined multiple times
❌ **Dead code** - Random proposal-related code mixed in voting engine
❌ **Missing error handling** - No validation of proposal existence
❌ **Thread-safety incomplete** - Lock doesn't cover all critical sections

### **3. Scalability Issues**

❌ **JSON file storage** - Won't scale beyond ~10K votes
❌ **No distributed consensus** - Single point of failure
❌ **No audit trail integration** - Missing Ineffable Ledger connection

***

## **Enhanced Production-Ready `voting_engine.py`**

Here's the refactored version with **BlissID integration, SoulSync vote weighting, anti-Sybil protection, and distributed ledger support**:

```python
# AuraFS Governance Module: voting_engine.py (Enhanced)
# Integrates: BlissID, SoulSync, ID.me, Quantum Signatures, Distributed Ledger

import threading
import json
import uuid
import time
import hashlib
from typing import List, Optional, Dict, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
from enum import Enum

# Import AuraFS modules (assume these exist in your codebase)
from .identity_verifier import IdentityVerifier
from .blissid_manager import BlissIDManager
from .soulsync_engine import SoulSyncEngine
from .audit_log import AuditLogger
from .consensus_integration import ConsensusClient


class VoteOption(Enum):
    YES = "yes"
    NO = "no"
    ABSTAIN = "abstain"
    

@dataclass
class Vote:
    """Enhanced vote with soul-binding and cryptographic proof"""
    id: str
    proposal_id: str
    bliss_id: str  # Soul-bound identity (replaces voter_id)
    option: str
    vote_weight: float  # 1.0-1.5 based on SoulSync coherence
    quantum_signature: str  # Dilithium5 signature
    soul_proof: Dict  # Zero-knowledge proof of unique soul
    metadata: Dict
    timestamp: int
    verified: bool = False
    ledger_tx_hash: Optional[str] = None
    
    def to_dict(self):
        return asdict(self)
    
    @staticmethod
    def from_dict(d: Dict) -> 'Vote':
        return Vote(**d)
    
    def compute_hash(self) -> str:
        """Cryptographic hash for audit trail"""
        data = f"{self.proposal_id}:{self.bliss_id}:{self.option}:{self.timestamp}"
        return hashlib.sha3_256(data.encode()).hexdigest()


class VotingEngine:
    """
    Production-grade voting engine with:
    - One-soul-per-vote enforcement via BlissID
    - Soul-coherence weighted voting via SoulSync
    - Quantum-safe cryptography
    - Distributed ledger integration
    - Complete audit trail
    """
    
    def __init__(
        self, 
        vote_db: str = "./governance/votes.json",
        identity_verifier: Optional[IdentityVerifier] = None,
        blissid_manager: Optional[BlissIDManager] = None,
        soulsync_engine: Optional[SoulSyncEngine] = None,
        consensus_client: Optional[ConsensusClient] = None,
        audit_logger: Optional[AuditLogger] = None
    ):
        self.vote_db = vote_db
        self.lock = threading.RLock()  # Reentrant lock for nested calls
        
        # Dependency injection for modularity
        self.identity_verifier = identity_verifier or IdentityVerifier()
        self.blissid_manager = blissid_manager or BlissIDManager()
        self.soulsync_engine = soulsync_engine or SoulSyncEngine()
        self.consensus_client = consensus_client or ConsensusClient()
        self.audit_logger = audit_logger or AuditLogger()
        
        # In-memory cache (write-through to disk + ledger)
        self._votes: Dict[str, Vote] = {}
        self._vote_index: Dict[str, Dict[str, str]] = {}  # proposal_id -> {bliss_id: vote_id}
        
        self._load()
    
    def _load(self):
        """Load votes from persistent storage"""
        try:
            with open(self.vote_db, "r") as f:
                raw = json.load(f)
                self._votes = {k: Vote.from_dict(v) for k, v in raw.items()}
                self._rebuild_index()
        except FileNotFoundError:
            self._votes = {}
            self._vote_index = {}
        except Exception as e:
            self.audit_logger.log_error(f"Failed to load votes: {e}")
            self._votes = {}
            self._vote_index = {}
    
    def _save(self):
        """Persist votes to disk (write-through cache)"""
        with self.lock:
            try:
                with open(self.vote_db, "w") as f:
                    d = {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self._votes.items()}
                    json.dump(d, f, indent=2)
            except Exception as e:
                self.audit_logger.log_error(f"Failed to save votes: {e}")
                raise
    
    def _rebuild_index(self):
        """Build proposal->bliss_id index for duplicate detection"""
        self._vote_index = {}
        for vote_id, vote in self._votes.items():
            if vote.proposal_id not in self._vote_index:
                self._vote_index[vote.proposal_id] = {}
            self._vote_index[vote.proposal_id][vote.bliss_id] = vote_id
    
    def cast_vote(
        self, 
        proposal_id: str, 
        bliss_id: str,
        option: str, 
        quantum_signature: str,
        soul_proof: Dict,
        metadata: Optional[Dict] = None
    ) -> Tuple[Optional[Vote], Optional[str]]:
        """
        Cast a vote with full soul-binding enforcement
        
        Returns:
            (Vote, None) on success
            (None, error_message) on failure
        """
        
        # Step 1: Validate BlissID exists and is active
        if not self.blissid_manager.verify_bliss_id(bliss_id):
            return None, f"Invalid or inactive BlissID: {bliss_id}"
        
        # Step 2: Verify quantum signature (proof of BlissID ownership)
        if not self.identity_verifier.verify_quantum_signature(
            bliss_id, quantum_signature, f"{proposal_id}:{option}"
        ):
            return None, "Invalid quantum signature"
        
        # Step 3: Verify zero-knowledge proof of unique soul
        if not self.identity_verifier.verify_soul_proof(bliss_id, soul_proof):
            return None, "Invalid soul proof - possible duplicate identity"
        
        # Step 4: Check for duplicate vote (ONE SOUL = ONE VOTE)
        with self.lock:
            if proposal_id in self._vote_index:
                if bliss_id in self._vote_index[proposal_id]:
                    existing_vote_id = self._vote_index[proposal_id][bliss_id]
                    return None, f"Soul has already voted on this proposal (vote_id: {existing_vote_id})"
        
        # Step 5: Validate proposal exists and is open for voting
        # (Assumes proposal_manager integration - add your validation here)
        # if not self.proposal_manager.is_open(proposal_id):
        #     return None, "Proposal is not open for voting"
        
        # Step 6: Calculate soul-coherence vote weight
        vote_weight = self.soulsync_engine.compute_vote_weight(bliss_id)
        
        # Step 7: Create vote object
        vote = Vote(
            id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
            proposal_id=proposal_id,
            bliss_id=bliss_id,
            option=option,
            vote_weight=vote_weight,
            quantum_signature=quantum_signature,
            soul_proof=soul_proof,
            metadata=metadata or {},
            timestamp=int(time.time()),
            verified=True
        )
        
        # Step 8: Submit to distributed ledger (consensus layer)
        try:
            tx_hash = self.consensus_client.submit_vote(vote.to_dict())
            vote.ledger_tx_hash = tx_hash
        except Exception as e:
            self.audit_logger.log_error(f"Consensus submission failed: {e}")
            return None, f"Failed to submit vote to ledger: {e}"
        
        # Step 9: Persist vote (write-through cache)
        with self.lock:
            self._votes[vote.id] = vote
            if proposal_id not in self._vote_index:
                self._vote_index[proposal_id] = {}
            self._vote_index[proposal_id][bliss_id] = vote.id
            self._save()
        
        # Step 10: Audit log
        self.audit_logger.log_vote_cast(
            vote_id=vote.id,
            proposal_id=proposal_id,
            bliss_id=bliss_id,
            vote_weight=vote_weight,
            ledger_tx=tx_hash
        )
        
        return vote, None
    
    def get_vote(self, vote_id: str) -> Optional[Vote]:
        """Retrieve vote by ID"""
        with self.lock:
            return self._votes.get(vote_id)
    
    def list_votes(
        self, 
        proposal_id: Optional[str] = None,
        bliss_id: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> List[Vote]:
        """
        List votes with optional filtering
        
        Args:
            proposal_id: Filter by proposal
            bliss_id: Filter by soul identity
        """
        with self.lock:
            votes = list(self._votes.values())
            
            if proposal_id:
                votes = [v for v in votes if v.proposal_id == proposal_id]
            
            if bliss_id:
                votes = [v for v in votes if v.bliss_id == bliss_id]
            
            return votes
    
    def tally_votes(self, proposal_id: str) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        Tally votes for a proposal with soul-weighted counting
        
        Returns:
            {
                "yes": 145.3,  # Total weighted votes
                "no": 87.6,
                "abstain": 23.1,
                "total_souls": 256,  # Unique voters
                "total_weight": 256.0  # Sum of all weights
            }
        """
        votes = self.list_votes(proposal_id=proposal_id)
        
        tally = {}
        total_weight = 0.0
        
        for vote in votes:
            if vote.option not in tally:
                tally[vote.option] = 0.0
            tally[vote.option] += vote.vote_weight
            total_weight += vote.vote_weight
        
        tally["total_souls"] = len(votes)
        tally["total_weight"] = total_weight
        
        return tally
    
    def verify_vote_integrity(self, vote_id: str) -> bool:
        """
        Verify vote hasn't been tampered with by checking:
        1. Quantum signature validity
        2. Ledger transaction exists
        3. Vote hash matches
        """
        vote = self.get_vote(vote_id)
        if not vote:
            return False
        
        # Check quantum signature
        if not self.identity_verifier.verify_quantum_signature(
            vote.bliss_id, 
            vote.quantum_signature, 
            f"{vote.proposal_id}:{vote.option}"
        ):
            return False
        
        # Check ledger transaction
        if vote.ledger_tx_hash:
            if not self.consensus_client.verify_transaction(vote.ledger_tx_hash):
                return False
        
        return True
    
    def get_soul_voting_history(self, bliss_id: str) -> List[Vote]:
        """Get all votes cast by a specific soul"""
        return self.list_votes(bliss_id=bliss_id)
    
    def has_voted(self, proposal_id: str, bliss_id: str) -> bool:
        """Check if soul has already voted on proposal"""
        with self.lock:
            return (proposal_id in self._vote_index and 
                    bliss_id in self._vote_index[proposal_id])


# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Initialize voting engine with all dependencies
    voting_engine = VotingEngine()
    
    # Cast a vote (with full BlissID verification)
    vote, error = voting_engine.cast_vote(
        proposal_id="prop-123",
        bliss_id="bliss:abc-def-123",
        option="yes",
        quantum_signature="a1b2c3d4...",  # Dilithium5 signature
        soul_proof={
            "commitment": "hash123...",
            "proof_type": "zk-SNARK-unique-human"
        },
        metadata={"voter_comment": "I support this proposal"}
    )
    
    if error:
        print(f"Vote failed: {error}")
    else:
        print(f"Vote cast successfully: {vote.id}")
        print(f"Vote weight: {vote.vote_weight}")
        print(f"Ledger TX: {vote.ledger_tx_hash}")
    
    # Tally votes
    results = voting_engine.tally_votes("prop-123")
    print(f"Voting results: {results}")
```

### **1. `identity_verifier.rs` - Quantum-Safe Identity Verification**

```rust
// /afs/governance/identity_verifier.rs
// Identity verification with BlissID, ID.me integration, and quantum signatures

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::{Arc, RwLock};
use serde::{Serialize, Deserialize};
use sha3::{Digest, Sha3_256};
use chrono::Utc;

// Post-quantum signature verification (Dilithium5)
use pqcrypto_dilithium::dilithium5;
use pqcrypto_traits::sign::*;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct BlissID {
    pub id: String,
    pub did: String,  // Decentralized Identifier from ID.me
    pub soul_hash: String,  // Biometric commitment hash
    pub created_at: i64,
    pub active: bool,
    pub soul_proof: SoulProof,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct SoulProof {
    pub commitment: String,
    pub proof_type: String,  // "zk-SNARK-unique-human"
    pub verifiable: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct IdentityVerifier {
    bliss_registry: Arc<RwLock<HashMap<String, BlissID>>>,
    public_keys: Arc<RwLock<HashMap<String, dilithium5::PublicKey>>>,
}

impl IdentityVerifier {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            bliss_registry: Arc::new(RwLock::new(HashMap::new())),
            public_keys: Arc::new(RwLock::new(HashMap::new())),
        }
    }

    /// Register a new BlissID (called after ID.me + ABIS verification)
    pub fn register_bliss_id(&self, bliss_id: BlissID, public_key: dilithium5::PublicKey) -> Result<(), String> {
        let mut registry = self.bliss_registry.write().unwrap();
        let mut keys = self.public_keys.write().unwrap();

        // Check for duplicate soul_hash (one soul per account)
        for existing in registry.values() {
            if existing.soul_hash == bliss_id.soul_hash {
                return Err(format!("Soul already registered: {}", existing.id));
            }
        }

        registry.insert(bliss_id.id.clone(), bliss_id.clone());
        keys.insert(bliss_id.id.clone(), public_key);

        log::info!("✅ BlissID registered: {}", bliss_id.id);
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Verify BlissID is active and valid
    pub fn verify_bliss_id(&self, bliss_id: &str) -> bool {
        let registry = self.bliss_registry.read().unwrap();
        registry.get(bliss_id)
            .map(|id| id.active)
            .unwrap_or(false)
    }

    /// Verify quantum signature (Dilithium5)
    pub fn verify_quantum_signature(
        &self,
        bliss_id: &str,
        message: &[u8],
        signature: &[u8],
    ) -> Result<bool, String> {
        let keys = self.public_keys.read().unwrap();

        let public_key = keys.get(bliss_id)
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("No public key for BlissID: {}", bliss_id))?;

        // Convert signature bytes to Dilithium5 signature
        let sig = dilithium5::DetachedSignature::from_bytes(signature)
            .map_err(|e| format!("Invalid signature format: {:?}", e))?;

        // Verify signature
        match dilithium5::verify_detached_signature(&sig, message, public_key) {
            Ok(_) => Ok(true),
            Err(_) => Ok(false),
        }
    }

    /// Verify zero-knowledge proof of unique soul
    pub fn verify_soul_proof(&self, bliss_id: &str, soul_proof: &SoulProof) -> bool {
        let registry = self.bliss_registry.read().unwrap();

        if let Some(registered_id) = registry.get(bliss_id) {
            // Verify commitment matches registered soul_hash
            let computed_commitment = self.compute_commitment(&registered_id.soul_hash, bliss_id);
            
            soul_proof.commitment == computed_commitment 
                && soul_proof.proof_type == "zk-SNARK-unique-human"
                && soul_proof.verifiable
        } else {
            false
        }
    }

    /// Compute commitment for zero-knowledge proof
    fn compute_commitment(&self, soul_hash: &str, bliss_id: &str) -> String {
        let mut hasher = Sha3_256::new();
        hasher.update(format!("{}:{}", bliss_id, soul_hash).as_bytes());
        format!("{:x}", hasher.finalize())
    }

    /// Get BlissID info
    pub fn get_bliss_id(&self, bliss_id: &str) -> Option<BlissID> {
        let registry = self.bliss_registry.read().unwrap();
        registry.get(bliss_id).cloned()
    }

    /// Deactivate BlissID (for security or policy violations)
    pub fn deactivate_bliss_id(&self, bliss_id: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        let mut registry = self.bliss_registry.write().unwrap();

        if let Some(id) = registry.get_mut(bliss_id) {
            id.active = false;
            log::warn!("🚫 BlissID deactivated: {}", bliss_id);
            Ok(())
        } else {
            Err(format!("BlissID not found: {}", bliss_id))
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_bliss_id_registration() {
        let verifier = IdentityVerifier::new();
        let (pk, _sk) = dilithium5::keypair();

        let bliss_id = BlissID {
            id: "bliss:test-123".to_string(),
            did: "did:example:abc123".to_string(),
            soul_hash: "hash123".to_string(),
            created_at: Utc::now().timestamp(),
            active: true,
            soul_proof: SoulProof {
                commitment: "commit123".to_string(),
                proof_type: "zk-SNARK-unique-human".to_string(),
                verifiable: true,
            },
        };

        assert!(verifier.register_bliss_id(bliss_id.clone(), pk).is_ok());
        assert!(verifier.verify_bliss_id(&bliss_id.id));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_duplicate_soul_prevention() {
        let verifier = IdentityVerifier::new();
        let (pk1, _) = dilithium5::keypair();
        let (pk2, _) = dilithium5::keypair();

        let bliss_id1 = BlissID {
            id: "bliss:user1".to_string(),
            did: "did:user1".to_string(),
            soul_hash: "same_soul_hash".to_string(),
            created_at: Utc::now().timestamp(),
            active: true,
            soul_proof: SoulProof {
                commitment: "c1".to_string(),
                proof_type: "zk-SNARK-unique-human".to_string(),
                verifiable: true,
            },
        };

        let bliss_id2 = BlissID {
            id: "bliss:user2".to_string(),
            did: "did:user2".to_string(),
            soul_hash: "same_soul_hash".to_string(),  // DUPLICATE
            created_at: Utc::now().timestamp(),
            active: true,
            soul_proof: SoulProof {
                commitment: "c2".to_string(),
                proof_type: "zk-SNARK-unique-human".to_string(),
                verifiable: true,
            },
        };

        assert!(verifier.register_bliss_id(bliss_id1, pk1).is_ok());
        assert!(verifier.register_bliss_id(bliss_id2, pk2).is_err());  // Should fail
    }
}
```

## **2. `consensus_integration.rs` - BFT Consensus + Ledger Anchoring**

```rust
// /afs/governance/consensus_integration.rs
// Distributed consensus integration for AuraFS governance
// Implements BFT consensus, vote validation, and ledger anchoring

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::{Arc, RwLock};
use serde::{Serialize, Deserialize};
use sha3::{Digest, Sha3_256};
use chrono::Utc;
use tokio::sync::mpsc;

// Post-quantum signature support
use pqcrypto_dilithium::dilithium5;
use pqcrypto_traits::sign::*;

/// Transaction types that can be submitted to consensus
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum GovernanceTransaction {
    VoteCast {
        vote_id: String,
        proposal_id: String,
        bliss_id: String,
        option: String,
        vote_weight: f64,
        signature: Vec<u8>,
        timestamp: i64,
    },
    ProposalCreated {
        proposal_id: String,
        title: String,
        creator_bliss_id: String,
        timestamp: i64,
    },
    ProposalFinalized {
        proposal_id: String,
        result: String,  // "approved", "rejected"
        tally: HashMap<String, f64>,
        timestamp: i64,
    },
}

/// Consensus node role
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum NodeRole {
    Leader,
    Validator,
    Observer,
}

/// Consensus state for a transaction
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ConsensusState {
    pub transaction_hash: String,
    pub votes: HashMap<String, bool>,  // node_id -> approve/reject
    pub finalized: bool,
    pub block_height: u64,
}

/// BFT Consensus Manager
pub struct ConsensusIntegration {
    node_id: String,
    role: Arc<RwLock<NodeRole>>,
    validators: Arc<RwLock<Vec<String>>>,  // List of validator node IDs
    pending_transactions: Arc<RwLock<HashMap<String, GovernanceTransaction>>>,
    consensus_states: Arc<RwLock<HashMap<String, ConsensusState>>>,
    ledger_client: Arc<dyn LedgerClient + Send + Sync>,
    tx_sender: mpsc::UnboundedSender<GovernanceTransaction>,
    tx_receiver: Arc<RwLock<mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<GovernanceTransaction>>>,
}

impl ConsensusIntegration {
    pub fn new(node_id: String, validators: Vec<String>, ledger_client: Arc<dyn LedgerClient + Send + Sync>) -> Self {
        let (tx_sender, tx_receiver) = mpsc::unbounded_channel();

        Self {
            node_id,
            role: Arc::new(RwLock::new(NodeRole::Validator)),
            validators: Arc::new(RwLock::new(validators)),
            pending_transactions: Arc::new(RwLock::new(HashMap::new())),
            consensus_states: Arc::new(RwLock::new(HashMap::new())),
            ledger_client,
            tx_sender,
            tx_receiver: Arc::new(RwLock::new(tx_receiver)),
        }
    }

    /// Submit governance transaction to consensus network
    pub async fn submit_transaction(&self, tx: GovernanceTransaction) -> Result<String, String> {
        let tx_hash = self.compute_transaction_hash(&tx);
        
        // Add to pending pool
        {
            let mut pending = self.pending_transactions.write().unwrap();
            pending.insert(tx_hash.clone(), tx.clone());
        }

        // Broadcast to validators (simplified - would use P2P networking)
        self.broadcast_transaction(tx.clone()).await?;

        // Initialize consensus state
        {
            let mut states = self.consensus_states.write().unwrap();
            states.insert(tx_hash.clone(), ConsensusState {
                transaction_hash: tx_hash.clone(),
                votes: HashMap::new(),
                finalized: false,
                block_height: 0,
            });
        }

        log::info!("📤 Transaction submitted to consensus: {}", tx_hash);
        Ok(tx_hash)
    }

    /// Validate and vote on a transaction (BFT consensus step)
    pub async fn validate_transaction(&self, tx_hash: &str, approve: bool) -> Result<(), String> {
        let validators = self.validators.read().unwrap();
        
        // Check if this node is a validator
        if !validators.contains(&self.node_id) {
            return Err("Node is not a validator".to_string());
        }

        // Record vote
        {
            let mut states = self.consensus_states.write().unwrap();
            if let Some(state) = states.get_mut(tx_hash) {
                state.votes.insert(self.node_id.clone(), approve);
                log::info!("🗳️  Node {} voted {} on tx {}", self.node_id, if approve { "APPROVE" } else { "REJECT" }, tx_hash);
            } else {
                return Err(format!("Transaction not found: {}", tx_hash));
            }
        }

        // Check if consensus reached (2/3+ approval required for BFT)
        self.check_consensus(tx_hash).await
    }

    /// Check if BFT consensus (2/3+ majority) is reached
    async fn check_consensus(&self, tx_hash: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        let validators = self.validators.read().unwrap();
        let validator_count = validators.len();
        let required_votes = (validator_count * 2) / 3 + 1;  // 2/3+ majority

        let mut states = self.consensus_states.write().unwrap();
        let state = states.get_mut(tx_hash)
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("Transaction not found: {}", tx_hash))?;

        if state.finalized {
            return Ok(());
        }

        let approve_count = state.votes.values().filter(|&&v| v).count();
        let reject_count = state.votes.values().filter(|&&v| !v).count();

        // Check if 2/3+ approved
        if approve_count >= required_votes {
            state.finalized = true;
            log::info!("✅ CONSENSUS REACHED (APPROVED): {} ({}/{})", tx_hash, approve_count, validator_count);
            
            // Anchor to ledger
            self.anchor_to_ledger(tx_hash).await?;
            return Ok(());
        }

        // Check if 1/3+ rejected (impossible to reach consensus)
        if reject_count > validator_count / 3 {
            state.finalized = true;
            log::warn!("❌ CONSENSUS FAILED (REJECTED): {} ({}/{})", tx_hash, reject_count, validator_count);
            return Err(format!("Transaction rejected by validators: {}", tx_hash));
        }

        // Still waiting for more votes
        log::debug!("⏳ Waiting for consensus: {} ({}/{} votes)", tx_hash, state.votes.len(), validator_count);
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Anchor finalized transaction to the Ineffable Ledger
    async fn anchor_to_ledger(&self, tx_hash: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
        let pending = self.pending_transactions.read().unwrap();
        let tx = pending.get(tx_hash)
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("Transaction not found in pending pool: {}", tx_hash))?;

        // Serialize transaction for ledger
        let tx_data = serde_json::to_string(tx)
            .map_err(|e| format!("Serialization error: {}", e))?;

        // Submit to ledger
        let ledger_tx_hash = self.ledger_client.append_transaction(tx_hash, &tx_data).await
            .map_err(|e| format!("Ledger append failed: {}", e))?;

        // Update block height
        {
            let mut states = self.consensus_states.write().unwrap();
            if let Some(state) = states.get_mut(tx_hash) {
                state.block_height = self.ledger_client.get_current_block_height().await;
            }
        }

        log::info!("⛓️  Transaction anchored to ledger: {} -> {}", tx_hash, ledger_tx_hash);
        Ok(ledger_tx_hash)
    }

    /// Broadcast transaction to all validators
    async fn broadcast_transaction(&self, tx: GovernanceTransaction) -> Result<(), String> {
        // In production, this would use P2P networking (libp2p, QUIC, etc.)
        // For now, simulate by sending to internal queue
        self.tx_sender.send(tx)
            .map_err(|e| format!("Broadcast failed: {}", e))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Compute deterministic hash for transaction
    fn compute_transaction_hash(&self, tx: &GovernanceTransaction) -> String {
        let tx_bytes = serde_json::to_vec(tx).unwrap();
        let mut hasher = Sha3_256::new();
        hasher.update(&tx_bytes);
        format!("{:x}", hasher.finalize())
    }

    /// Verify transaction integrity against ledger
    pub async fn verify_transaction(&self, tx_hash: &str) -> Result<bool, String> {
        self.ledger_client.verify_transaction(tx_hash).await
    }

    /// Get consensus state for a transaction
    pub fn get_consensus_state(&self, tx_hash: &str) -> Option<ConsensusState> {
        let states = self.consensus_states.read().unwrap();
        states.get(tx_hash).cloned()
    }

    /// Promote node to leader (for leader election)
    pub fn promote_to_leader(&self) {
        let mut role = self.role.write().unwrap();
        *role = NodeRole::Leader;
        log::info!("👑 Node {} promoted to LEADER", self.node_id);
    }

    /// Demote node to validator
    pub fn demote_to_validator(&self) {
        let mut role = self.role.write().unwrap();
        *role = NodeRole::Validator;
        log::info!("🔻 Node {} demoted to VALIDATOR", self.node_id);
    }
}

/// Ledger client trait (abstraction for different ledger backends)
#[async_trait::async_trait]
pub trait LedgerClient {
    async fn append_transaction(&self, tx_hash: &str, tx_data: &str) -> Result<String, String>;
    async fn get_current_block_height(&self) -> u64;
    async fn verify_transaction(&self, tx_hash: &str) -> Result<bool, String>;
}

/// Mock ledger client for testing
pub struct MockLedgerClient {
    transactions: Arc<RwLock<HashMap<String, String>>>,
    block_height: Arc<RwLock<u64>>,
}

impl MockLedgerClient {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            transactions: Arc::new(RwLock::new(HashMap::new())),
            block_height: Arc::new(RwLock::new(0)),
        }
    }
}

#[async_trait::async_trait]
impl LedgerClient for MockLedgerClient {
    async fn append_transaction(&self, tx_hash: &str, tx_data: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
        let mut txs = self.transactions.write().unwrap();
        txs.insert(tx_hash.to_string(), tx_data.to_string());
        
        let mut height = self.block_height.write().unwrap();
        *height += 1;
        
        Ok(format!("ledger_tx_{}", *height))
    }

    async fn get_current_block_height(&self) -> u64 {
        *self.block_height.read().unwrap()
    }

    async fn verify_transaction(&self, tx_hash: &str) -> Result<bool, String> {
        let txs = self.transactions.read().unwrap();
        Ok(txs.contains_key(tx_hash))
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]

        let validators = vec!["node1".to_string(), "node2".to_string(), "node3".to_string()];

            timestamp: Utc::now().timestamp(),
        };

        let tx_hash = consensus.submit_transaction(tx).await.unwrap();
        
        // Simulate validator votes (2/3 approve)
        consensus.validate_transaction(&tx_hash, true).await.unwrap();  // node1
        consensus.validate_transaction(&tx_hash, true).await.unwrap();  // node2 (consensus reached)

        // Verify finalized
        let state = consensus.get_consensus_state(&tx_hash).unwrap();
        assert!(state.finalized);
        assert_eq!(state.block_height, 1);
    }
}
```

***

## **Key Features**

### **1. Byzantine Fault Tolerant (BFT) Consensus**

- **2/3+ majority rule** - Requires 67%+ validator approval
- **Byzantine resilience** - Tolerates up to 33% malicious/faulty validators
- **Vote tracking** - Each validator records approve/reject decision


### **2. Governance Transaction Types**

```rust
pub enum GovernanceTransaction {
    VoteCast { ... },           // Vote submission
    ProposalCreated { ... },    // New proposal
    ProposalFinalized { ... },  // Voting complete
}
```


### **3. Distributed Validation**

- **Validator nodes** - Approved authorities that vote on transactions
- **Leader election support** - Promote/demote node roles
- **Consensus state tracking** - Real-time vote tallying


### **4. Immutable Ledger Anchoring**

- **LedgerClient trait** - Abstraction for blockchain/distributed ledger
- **Automatic anchoring** - Finalized transactions written to ledger
- **Block height tracking** - Maintains chronological ordering


### **5. Transaction Integrity**

- **SHA3-256 hashing** - Deterministic transaction identifiers
- **Verification API** - Confirm transaction exists on ledger
- **Audit trail** - Every consensus decision recorded


### **6. Async/Concurrent Design**

- **Tokio async runtime** - Non-blocking I/O
- **Lock-free reads** - RwLock for concurrent access
- **Message passing** - `mpsc` channels for transaction broadcast

***

## **Integration with Voting Engine**

The `consensus_integration.rs` module connects to your `voting_engine.py` (or Rust port) like this:

```rust
// Example: Submit vote to consensus from voting engine

use crate::consensus_integration::{ConsensusIntegration, GovernanceTransaction};

pub async fn cast_vote_with_consensus(
    consensus: &ConsensusIntegration,
    vote_id: String,
    proposal_id: String,
    bliss_id: String,
    option: String,
    vote_weight: f64,
    signature: Vec<u8>,
) -> Result<String, String> {
    let tx = GovernanceTransaction::VoteCast {
        vote_id,
        proposal_id,
        bliss_id,
        option,
        vote_weight,
        signature,
        timestamp: chrono::Utc::now().timestamp(),
    };

    // Submit to distributed consensus
    let tx_hash = consensus.submit_transaction(tx).await?;
    
    Ok(tx_hash)
}
```

## **3. `voting_engine.rs` - Production Rust Voting Engine**

```rust
// /afs/governance/voting_engine.rs
// Production-grade voting engine with BlissID integration and consensus anchoring

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::{Arc, RwLock};
use serde::{Serialize, Deserialize};
use sha3::{Digest, Sha3_256};
use chrono::Utc;
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::identity_verifier::{IdentityVerifier, SoulProof};
use crate::consensus_integration::{ConsensusIntegration, GovernanceTransaction};

/// Vote options
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
pub enum VoteOption {
    Yes,
    No,
    Abstain,
}

/// Individual vote record
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Vote {
    pub id: String,
    pub proposal_id: String,
    pub bliss_id: String,
    pub option: VoteOption,
    pub vote_weight: f64,
    pub quantum_signature: Vec<u8>,
    pub soul_proof: SoulProof,
    pub metadata: HashMap<String, String>,
    pub timestamp: i64,
    pub verified: bool,
    pub ledger_tx_hash: Option<String>,
}

impl Vote {
    pub fn compute_hash(&self) -> String {
        let data = format!("{}:{}:{:?}:{}", 
            self.proposal_id, self.bliss_id, self.option, self.timestamp);
        let mut hasher = Sha3_256::new();
        hasher.update(data.as_bytes());
        format!("{:x}", hasher.finalize())
    }
}

/// Vote tally results
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct VoteTally {
    pub yes: f64,
    pub no: f64,
    pub abstain: f64,
    pub total_souls: usize,
    pub total_weight: f64,
}

/// Voting engine with distributed consensus
pub struct VotingEngine {
    votes: Arc<RwLock<HashMap<String, Vote>>>,
    vote_index: Arc<RwLock<HashMap<String, HashMap<String, String>>>>, // proposal_id -> {bliss_id: vote_id}
    identity_verifier: Arc<IdentityVerifier>,
    consensus: Arc<ConsensusIntegration>,
}

impl VotingEngine {
    pub fn new(
        identity_verifier: Arc<IdentityVerifier>,
        consensus: Arc<ConsensusIntegration>,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            votes: Arc::new(RwLock::new(HashMap::new())),
            vote_index: Arc::new(RwLock::new(HashMap::new())),
            identity_verifier,
            consensus,
        }
    }

    /// Cast a vote with full BlissID verification and consensus anchoring
    pub async fn cast_vote(
        &self,
        proposal_id: String,
        bliss_id: String,
        option: VoteOption,
        quantum_signature: Vec<u8>,
        soul_proof: SoulProof,
        metadata: Option<HashMap<String, String>>,
    ) -> Result<Vote, String> {
        
        // Step 1: Validate BlissID is active
        if !self.identity_verifier.verify_bliss_id(&bliss_id) {
            return Err(format!("Invalid or inactive BlissID: {}", bliss_id));
        }

        // Step 2: Verify quantum signature
        let message = format!("{}:{:?}", proposal_id, option);
        let signature_valid = self.identity_verifier
            .verify_quantum_signature(&bliss_id, message.as_bytes(), &quantum_signature)
            .map_err(|e| format!("Signature verification failed: {}", e))?;

        if !signature_valid {
            return Err("Invalid quantum signature".to_string());
        }

        // Step 3: Verify soul proof (zero-knowledge proof of uniqueness)
        if !self.identity_verifier.verify_soul_proof(&bliss_id, &soul_proof) {
            return Err("Invalid soul proof - possible duplicate identity".to_string());
        }

        // Step 4: Check for duplicate vote (ONE SOUL = ONE VOTE)
        {
            let index = self.vote_index.read().unwrap();
            if let Some(proposal_votes) = index.get(&proposal_id) {
                if let Some(existing_vote_id) = proposal_votes.get(&bliss_id) {
                    return Err(format!("Soul has already voted on this proposal (vote_id: {})", existing_vote_id));
                }
            }
        }

        // Step 5: Calculate soul-coherence vote weight (placeholder - integrate SoulSync)
        let vote_weight = self.compute_vote_weight(&bliss_id).await?;

        // Step 6: Create vote record
        let vote_id = Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
        let vote = Vote {
            id: vote_id.clone(),
            proposal_id: proposal_id.clone(),
            bliss_id: bliss_id.clone(),
            option: option.clone(),
            vote_weight,
            quantum_signature,
            soul_proof,
            metadata: metadata.unwrap_or_default(),
            timestamp: Utc::now().timestamp(),
            verified: true,
            ledger_tx_hash: None,
        };

        // Step 7: Submit to distributed consensus
        let consensus_tx = GovernanceTransaction::VoteCast {
            vote_id: vote_id.clone(),
            proposal_id: proposal_id.clone(),
            bliss_id: bliss_id.clone(),
            option: format!("{:?}", option),
            vote_weight,
            signature: vote.quantum_signature.clone(),
            timestamp: vote.timestamp,
        };

        let tx_hash = self.consensus.submit_transaction(consensus_tx).await
            .map_err(|e| format!("Consensus submission failed: {}", e))?;

        // Step 8: Update vote with ledger transaction hash
        let mut final_vote = vote;
        final_vote.ledger_tx_hash = Some(tx_hash.clone());

        // Step 9: Store vote in engine
        {
            let mut votes = self.votes.write().unwrap();
            votes.insert(vote_id.clone(), final_vote.clone());

            // Update index
            let mut index = self.vote_index.write().unwrap();
            index.entry(proposal_id.clone())
                .or_insert_with(HashMap::new)
                .insert(bliss_id.clone(), vote_id.clone());
        }

        log::info!("✅ Vote cast successfully: {} (weight: {}, ledger: {})", 
            vote_id, vote_weight, tx_hash);

        Ok(final_vote)
    }

    /// Compute vote weight based on SoulSync coherence
    async fn compute_vote_weight(&self, bliss_id: &str) -> Result<f64, String> {
        // Placeholder - integrate with soulsync_engine
        // Base weight is 1.0, bonus 0-0.5 based on coherence
        
        // For now, return base weight
        // TODO: Integrate SoulSyncEngine
        // let coherence = self.soulsync_engine.compute_coherence(bliss_id).await?;
        // Ok(1.0 + (0.5 * coherence))
        
        Ok(1.0)
    }

    /// Get a specific vote by ID
    pub fn get_vote(&self, vote_id: &str) -> Option<Vote> {
        let votes = self.votes.read().unwrap();
        votes.get(vote_id).cloned()
    }

    /// List all votes for a proposal
    pub fn list_votes(&self, proposal_id: Option<String>) -> Vec<Vote> {
        let votes = self.votes.read().unwrap();
        
        if let Some(prop_id) = proposal_id {
            votes.values()
                .filter(|v| v.proposal_id == prop_id)
                .cloned()
                .collect()
        } else {
            votes.values().cloned().collect()
        }
    }

    /// Check if a soul has voted on a proposal
    pub fn has_voted(&self, proposal_id: &str, bliss_id: &str) -> bool {
        let index = self.vote_index.read().unwrap();
        index.get(proposal_id)
            .and_then(|votes| votes.get(bliss_id))
            .is_some()
    }

    /// Tally votes for a proposal with soul-weighted counting
    pub fn tally_votes(&self, proposal_id: &str) -> VoteTally {
        let votes = self.list_votes(Some(proposal_id.to_string()));
        
        let mut tally = VoteTally {
            yes: 0.0,
            no: 0.0,
            abstain: 0.0,
            total_souls: votes.len(),
            total_weight: 0.0,
        };

        for vote in votes {
            match vote.option {
                VoteOption::Yes => tally.yes += vote.vote_weight,
                VoteOption::No => tally.no += vote.vote_weight,
                VoteOption::Abstain => tally.abstain += vote.vote_weight,
            }
            tally.total_weight += vote.vote_weight;
        }

        tally
    }

    /// Verify vote integrity against ledger
    pub async fn verify_vote_integrity(&self, vote_id: &str) -> Result<bool, String> {
        let vote = self.get_vote(vote_id)
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("Vote not found: {}", vote_id))?;

        // Step 1: Verify quantum signature
        let message = format!("{}:{:?}", vote.proposal_id, vote.option);
        let sig_valid = self.identity_verifier
            .verify_quantum_signature(&vote.bliss_id, message.as_bytes(), &vote.quantum_signature)
            .map_err(|e| format!("Signature verification failed: {}", e))?;

        if !sig_valid {
            return Ok(false);
        }

        // Step 2: Verify ledger transaction exists
        if let Some(tx_hash) = &vote.ledger_tx_hash {
            let ledger_valid = self.consensus.verify_transaction(tx_hash).await
                .map_err(|e| format!("Ledger verification failed: {}", e))?;
            
            if !ledger_valid {
                return Ok(false);
            }
        }

        Ok(true)
    }

    /// Get voting history for a specific soul
    pub fn get_soul_voting_history(&self, bliss_id: &str) -> Vec<Vote> {
        let votes = self.votes.read().unwrap();
        votes.values()
            .filter(|v| v.bliss_id == bliss_id)
            .cloned()
            .collect()
    }

    /// Get vote statistics for a proposal
    pub fn get_vote_statistics(&self, proposal_id: &str) -> HashMap<String, serde_json::Value> {
        use serde_json::json;

        let tally = self.tally_votes(proposal_id);
        let votes = self.list_votes(Some(proposal_id.to_string()));

        let avg_weight = if tally.total_souls > 0 {
            tally.total_weight / tally.total_souls as f64
        } else {
            0.0
        };

        let approval_rate = if tally.total_weight > 0.0 {
            (tally.yes / tally.total_weight) * 100.0
        } else {
            0.0
        };

        let mut stats = HashMap::new();
        stats.insert("total_votes".to_string(), json!(tally.total_souls));
        stats.insert("total_weight".to_string(), json!(tally.total_weight));
        stats.insert("yes_weight".to_string(), json!(tally.yes));
        stats.insert("no_weight".to_string(), json!(tally.no));
        stats.insert("abstain_weight".to_string(), json!(tally.abstain));
        stats.insert("average_weight".to_string(), json!(avg_weight));
        stats.insert("approval_rate_percent".to_string(), json!(approval_rate));
        stats.insert("unique_souls".to_string(), json!(tally.total_souls));

        stats
    }

    /// Export votes to JSON for audit
    pub fn export_votes(&self, proposal_id: Option<String>) -> Result<String, String> {
        let votes = self.list_votes(proposal_id);
        serde_json::to_string_pretty(&votes)
            .map_err(|e| format!("Export failed: {}", e))
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::identity_verifier::BlissID;
    use crate::consensus_integration::MockLedgerClient;
    use pqcrypto_dilithium::dilithium5;
    use pqcrypto_traits::sign::*;

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn test_vote_casting() {
        // Setup
        let identity_verifier = Arc::new(IdentityVerifier::new());
        let ledger = Arc::new(MockLedgerClient::new());
        let validators = vec!["node1".to_string()];
        let consensus = Arc::new(ConsensusIntegration::new(
            "node1".to_string(),
            validators,
            ledger,
        ));
        let voting_engine = VotingEngine::new(identity_verifier.clone(), consensus.clone());

        // Register BlissID
        let (pk, sk) = dilithium5::keypair();
        let bliss_id = BlissID {
            id: "bliss:test-123".to_string(),
            did: "did:test".to_string(),
            soul_hash: "hash123".to_string(),
            created_at: Utc::now().timestamp(),
            active: true,
            soul_proof: SoulProof {
                commitment: "commit123".to_string(),
                proof_type: "zk-SNARK-unique-human".to_string(),
                verifiable: true,
            },
        };
        identity_verifier.register_bliss_id(bliss_id.clone(), pk).unwrap();

        // Sign vote message
        let message = format!("prop-123:{:?}", VoteOption::Yes);
        let signature = dilithium5::detached_sign(message.as_bytes(), &sk);

        // Cast vote
        let vote = voting_engine.cast_vote(
            "prop-123".to_string(),
            bliss_id.id.clone(),
            VoteOption::Yes,
            signature.as_bytes().to_vec(),
            bliss_id.soul_proof.clone(),
            None,
        ).await.unwrap();

        assert_eq!(vote.option, VoteOption::Yes);
        assert!(vote.verified);
        assert!(vote.ledger_tx_hash.is_some());
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn test_duplicate_vote_prevention() {
        let identity_verifier = Arc::new(IdentityVerifier::new());
        let ledger = Arc::new(MockLedgerClient::new());
        let consensus = Arc::new(ConsensusIntegration::new(
            "node1".to_string(),
            vec!["node1".to_string()],
            ledger,
        ));
        let voting_engine = VotingEngine::new(identity_verifier.clone(), consensus.clone());

        // Register BlissID
        let (pk, sk) = dilithium5::keypair();
        let bliss_id = BlissID {
            id: "bliss:test-456".to_string(),
            did: "did:test2".to_string(),
            soul_hash: "hash456".to_string(),
            created_at: Utc::now().timestamp(),
            active: true,
            soul_proof: SoulProof {
                commitment: "commit456".to_string(),
                proof_type: "zk-SNARK-unique-human".to_string(),
                verifiable: true,
            },
        };
        identity_verifier.register_bliss_id(bliss_id.clone(), pk).unwrap();

        // Cast first vote
        let message1 = format!("prop-999:{:?}", VoteOption::Yes);
        let sig1 = dilithium5::detached_sign(message1.as_bytes(), &sk);
        voting_engine.cast_vote(
            "prop-999".to_string(),
            bliss_id.id.clone(),
            VoteOption::Yes,
            sig1.as_bytes().to_vec(),
            bliss_id.soul_proof.clone(),
            None,
        ).await.unwrap();

        // Try to vote again (should fail)
        let message2 = format!("prop-999:{:?}", VoteOption::No);
        let sig2 = dilithium5::detached_sign(message2.as_bytes(), &sk);
        let result = voting_engine.cast_vote(
            "prop-999".to_string(),
            bliss_id.id.clone(),
            VoteOption::No,
            sig2.as_bytes().to_vec(),
            bliss_id.soul_proof.clone(),
            None,
        ).await;

        assert!(result.is_err());
        assert!(result.unwrap_err().contains("already voted"));
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn test_vote_tallying() {
        let identity_verifier = Arc::new(IdentityVerifier::new());
        let ledger = Arc::new(MockLedgerClient::new());
        let consensus = Arc::new(ConsensusIntegration::new(
            "node1".to_string(),
            vec!["node1".to_string()],
            ledger,
        ));
        let voting_engine = VotingEngine::new(identity_verifier.clone(), consensus.clone());

        // Register 3 voters
        for i in 1..=3 {
            let (pk, sk) = dilithium5::keypair();
            let bliss_id = BlissID {
                id: format!("bliss:voter{}", i),
                did: format!("did:voter{}", i),
                soul_hash: format!("hash{}", i),
                created_at: Utc::now().timestamp(),
                active: true,
                soul_proof: SoulProof {
                    commitment: format!("commit{}", i),
                    proof_type: "zk-SNARK-unique-human".to_string(),
                    verifiable: true,
                },
            };
            identity_verifier.register_bliss_id(bliss_id.clone(), pk).unwrap();

            // Cast vote
            let option = if i == 1 { VoteOption::Yes } else { VoteOption::No };
            let message = format!("prop-tally:{:?}", option);
            let sig = dilithium5::detached_sign(message.as_bytes(), &sk);
            
            voting_engine.cast_vote(
                "prop-tally".to_string(),
                bliss_id.id.clone(),
                option,
                sig.as_bytes().to_vec(),
                bliss_id.soul_proof.clone(),
                None,
            ).await.unwrap();
        }

        // Tally results
        let tally = voting_engine.tally_votes("prop-tally");
        assert_eq!(tally.total_souls, 3);
        assert_eq!(tally.yes, 1.0);
        assert_eq!(tally.no, 2.0);
    }
}
```

## **Integration Example**

```rust
// Example: Using VotingEngine in your governance API

use afs::governance::{VotingEngine, VoteOption, IdentityVerifier, ConsensusIntegration};

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() {
    let identity_verifier = Arc::new(IdentityVerifier::new());
    let consensus = Arc::new(ConsensusIntegration::new(
        "node1".to_string(),
        vec!["node1".to_string(), "node2".to_string(), "node3".to_string()],
        Arc::new(ProductionLedgerClient::new()),
    ));
    
    let voting_engine = Arc::new(VotingEngine::new(
        identity_verifier.clone(),
        consensus.clone(),
    ));

    // Cast vote
    let vote = voting_engine.cast_vote(
        "prop-123".to_string(),
        "bliss:alice".to_string(),
        VoteOption::Yes,
        signature_bytes,
        soul_proof,
        Some(metadata),
    ).await.unwrap();

    println!("✅ Vote cast: {} (weight: {})", vote.id, vote.vote_weight);

    // Tally results
    let tally = voting_engine.tally_votes("prop-123");
    println!("📊 Results: YES={} NO={} ({}% approval)", 
        tally.yes, tally.no, 
        (tally.yes / tally.total_weight * 100.0));
}
```
