## **4. `proposal_manager.rs` - Proposal Lifecycle State Machine**

```rust
//! Governance proposal lifecycle management for AuraFS
//! f0rg3d in l0v3 by Ross Edwards & Aurphyx

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::{Arc, RwLock};
use serde::{Serialize, Deserialize};
use chrono::{Utc, Duration};
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::models::{Proposal, ProposalStatus, ProposalType};
use crate::voting_engine::{VotingEngine, VoteTally};
use crate::audit_log::AuditLogger;

/// Proposal lifecycle manager with state machine
pub struct ProposalManager {
    proposals: Arc<RwLock<HashMap<String, Proposal>>>,
    voting_engine: Arc<VotingEngine>,
    audit_logger: Arc<AuditLogger>,
    config: ProposalConfig,
}

/// Configuration for proposal system
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ProposalConfig {
    pub min_voting_period_hours: i64,
    pub max_voting_period_hours: i64,
    pub quorum_percentage: f64,        // e.g., 10.0 = 10% of eligible voters must participate
    pub approval_threshold: f64,        // e.g., 0.67 = 67% approval required
}

impl Default for ProposalConfig {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            min_voting_period_hours: 24,
            max_voting_period_hours: 168,  // 7 days
            quorum_percentage: 10.0,
            approval_threshold: 0.67,
        }
    }
}

impl ProposalManager {
    pub fn new(
        voting_engine: Arc<VotingEngine>,
        audit_logger: Arc<AuditLogger>,
        config: Option<ProposalConfig>,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            proposals: Arc::new(RwLock::new(HashMap::new())),
            voting_engine,
            audit_logger,
            config: config.unwrap_or_default(),
        }
    }

    /// Create a new proposal
    pub fn create_proposal(
        &self,
        creator_bliss_id: String,
        title: String,
        description: String,
        proposal_type: ProposalType,
        voting_period_hours: i64,
        metadata: Option<HashMap<String, String>>,
    ) -> Result<Proposal, String> {
        // Validate voting period
        if voting_period_hours < self.config.min_voting_period_hours {
            return Err(format!("Voting period too short (min: {} hours)", 
                self.config.min_voting_period_hours));
        }
        if voting_period_hours > self.config.max_voting_period_hours {
            return Err(format!("Voting period too long (max: {} hours)", 
                self.config.max_voting_period_hours));
        }

        let now = Utc::now().timestamp();
        let voting_end = now + (voting_period_hours * 3600);

        let proposal = Proposal {
            id: format!("prop-{}", Uuid::new_v4()),
            title: title.clone(),
            description: description.clone(),
            creator_bliss_id: creator_bliss_id.clone(),
            proposal_type,
            status: ProposalStatus::Draft,
            created_at: now,
            updated_at: now,
            voting_start: None,
            voting_end: Some(voting_end),
            metadata: metadata.unwrap_or_default(),
        };

        // Store proposal
        {
            let mut proposals = self.proposals.write().unwrap();
            proposals.insert(proposal.id.clone(), proposal.clone());
        }

        // Audit log
        self.audit_logger.log_event(
            "proposal_created",
            &format!("id={}, creator={}, title={}", proposal.id, creator_bliss_id, title),
        );

        log::info!("📝 Proposal created: {} by {}", proposal.id, creator_bliss_id);
        Ok(proposal)
    }

    /// Submit proposal for voting (transition Draft → Active)
    pub fn submit_proposal(&self, proposal_id: &str) -> Result<Proposal, String> {
        let mut proposals = self.proposals.write().unwrap();
        let proposal = proposals.get_mut(proposal_id)
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("Proposal not found: {}", proposal_id))?;

        // Check current status
        if proposal.status != ProposalStatus::Draft {
            return Err(format!("Proposal is not in draft status: {:?}", proposal.status));
        }

        // Transition to Active
        proposal.status = ProposalStatus::Active;
        proposal.voting_start = Some(Utc::now().timestamp());
        proposal.updated_at = Utc::now().timestamp();

        self.audit_logger.log_event(
            "proposal_submitted",
            &format!("id={}, voting_start={}", proposal_id, proposal.voting_start.unwrap()),
        );

        log::info!("✅ Proposal submitted for voting: {}", proposal_id);
        Ok(proposal.clone())
    }

    /// Close voting and finalize proposal
    pub fn finalize_proposal(&self, proposal_id: &str) -> Result<Proposal, String> {
        let mut proposals = self.proposals.write().unwrap();
        let proposal = proposals.get_mut(proposal_id)
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("Proposal not found: {}", proposal_id))?;

        // Check if voting period ended
        if let Some(voting_end) = proposal.voting_end {
            if Utc::now().timestamp() < voting_end {
                return Err(format!("Voting period not yet ended (ends: {})", voting_end));
            }
        }

        // Get vote tally
        let tally = self.voting_engine.tally_votes(proposal_id);

        // Check quorum
        let quorum_met = self.check_quorum(&tally)?;
        if !quorum_met {
            proposal.status = ProposalStatus::Rejected;
            proposal.updated_at = Utc::now().timestamp();
            
            self.audit_logger.log_event(
                "proposal_rejected_quorum",
                &format!("id={}, votes={}, quorum_required={}%", 
                    proposal_id, tally.total_souls, self.config.quorum_percentage),
            );

            return Ok(proposal.clone());
        }

        // Check approval threshold
        let approval_rate = tally.yes / tally.total_weight;
        let approved = approval_rate >= self.config.approval_threshold;

        proposal.status = if approved { 
            ProposalStatus::Approved 
        } else { 
            ProposalStatus::Rejected 
        };
        proposal.updated_at = Utc::now().timestamp();

        self.audit_logger.log_event(
            if approved { "proposal_approved" } else { "proposal_rejected" },
            &format!("id={}, approval_rate={:.2}%, yes={}, no={}, abstain={}", 
                proposal_id, approval_rate * 100.0, tally.yes, tally.no, tally.abstain),
        );

        log::info!("🏁 Proposal finalized: {} - {:?}", proposal_id, proposal.status);
        Ok(proposal.clone())
    }

    /// Check if quorum is met
    fn check_quorum(&self, tally: &VoteTally) -> Result<bool, String> {
        // TODO: Integrate with total eligible voters count
        // For now, assume quorum is met if minimum votes received
        let min_votes = 3;  // Placeholder
        Ok(tally.total_souls >= min_votes)
    }

    /// Cancel a proposal (only in Draft status)
    pub fn cancel_proposal(&self, proposal_id: &str, bliss_id: &str) -> Result<Proposal, String> {
        let mut proposals = self.proposals.write().unwrap();
        let proposal = proposals.get_mut(proposal_id)
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("Proposal not found: {}", proposal_id))?;

        // Check authorization (only creator can cancel)
        if proposal.creator_bliss_id != bliss_id {
            return Err("Only proposal creator can cancel".to_string());
        }

        // Check status
        if proposal.status != ProposalStatus::Draft {
            return Err(format!("Cannot cancel proposal in status: {:?}", proposal.status));
        }

        proposal.status = ProposalStatus::Cancelled;
        proposal.updated_at = Utc::now().timestamp();

        self.audit_logger.log_event(
            "proposal_cancelled",
            &format!("id={}, creator={}", proposal_id, bliss_id),
        );

        Ok(proposal.clone())
    }

    /// Get proposal by ID
    pub fn get_proposal(&self, proposal_id: &str) -> Option<Proposal> {
        let proposals = self.proposals.read().unwrap();
        proposals.get(proposal_id).cloned()
    }

    /// List all proposals (with optional status filter)
    pub fn list_proposals(&self, status: Option<ProposalStatus>) -> Vec<Proposal> {
        let proposals = self.proposals.read().unwrap();
        
        if let Some(filter_status) = status {
            proposals.values()
                .filter(|p| p.status == filter_status)
                .cloned()
                .collect()
        } else {
            proposals.values().cloned().collect()
        }
    }

    /// Get proposals by creator
    pub fn get_proposals_by_creator(&self, bliss_id: &str) -> Vec<Proposal> {
        let proposals = self.proposals.read().unwrap();
        proposals.values()
            .filter(|p| p.creator_bliss_id == bliss_id)
            .cloned()
            .collect()
    }

    /// Check if voting is currently open for a proposal
    pub fn is_voting_open(&self, proposal_id: &str) -> bool {
        if let Some(proposal) = self.get_proposal(proposal_id) {
            if proposal.status != ProposalStatus::Active {
                return false;
            }

            if let Some(voting_end) = proposal.voting_end {
                return Utc::now().timestamp() < voting_end;
            }
        }
        false
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::identity_verifier::IdentityVerifier;
    use crate::consensus_integration::{ConsensusIntegration, MockLedgerClient};

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn test_proposal_lifecycle() {
        let identity_verifier = Arc::new(IdentityVerifier::new());
        let ledger = Arc::new(MockLedgerClient::new());
        let consensus = Arc::new(ConsensusIntegration::new(
            "node1".to_string(),
            vec!["node1".to_string()],
            ledger,
        ));
        let voting_engine = Arc::new(VotingEngine::new(identity_verifier, consensus));
        let audit_logger = Arc::new(AuditLogger::new());
        let manager = ProposalManager::new(voting_engine, audit_logger, None);

        // Create proposal
        let proposal = manager.create_proposal(
            "bliss:creator".to_string(),
            "Test Proposal".to_string(),
            "This is a test".to_string(),
            ProposalType::NetworkUpgrade,
            48,
            None,
        ).unwrap();

        assert_eq!(proposal.status, ProposalStatus::Draft);

        // Submit for voting
        let active_proposal = manager.submit_proposal(&proposal.id).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(active_proposal.status, ProposalStatus::Active);
        assert!(manager.is_voting_open(&proposal.id));
    }
}
```

## **5. `policy_enforcer.rs` - ACL \& Network Policy Enforcement**

```rust
//! Policy enforcement engine for AuraFS governance decisions
//! f0rg3d in l0v3 by Ross Edwards & Aurphyx

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::{Arc, RwLock};
use serde::{Serialize, Deserialize};

use crate::models::{Proposal, ProposalType};
use crate::audit_log::AuditLogger;

/// Policy enforcement actions
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum PolicyAction {
    UpdateACL { user_id: String, shard_id: String, permissions: Vec<String> },
    ModifyNetworkRule { rule_id: String, action: String },
    UpgradeProtocol { version: String },
    ModifyQuorum { new_percentage: f64 },
    BanNode { node_id: String, reason: String },
}

/// Policy enforcement result
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct EnforcementResult {
    pub success: bool,
    pub message: String,
    pub timestamp: i64,
}

/// Policy enforcement engine
pub struct PolicyEnforcer {
    active_policies: Arc<RwLock<HashMap<String, PolicyAction>>>,
    audit_logger: Arc<AuditLogger>,
}

impl PolicyEnforcer {
    pub fn new(audit_logger: Arc<AuditLogger>) -> Self {
        Self {
            active_policies: Arc::new(RwLock::new(HashMap::new())),
            audit_logger,
        }
    }

    /// Enforce an approved proposal
    pub fn enforce_proposal(&self, proposal: &Proposal) -> Result<EnforcementResult, String> {
        // Extract policy action from proposal metadata
        let action = self.parse_policy_action(proposal)?;

        // Execute action based on type
        let result = match &action {
            PolicyAction::UpdateACL { user_id, shard_id, permissions } => {
                self.apply_acl_update(user_id, shard_id, permissions)?
            }
            PolicyAction::ModifyNetworkRule { rule_id, action: rule_action } => {
                self.apply_network_rule(rule_id, rule_action)?
            }
            PolicyAction::UpgradeProtocol { version } => {
                self.apply_protocol_upgrade(version)?
            }
            PolicyAction::ModifyQuorum { new_percentage } => {
                self.apply_quorum_change(*new_percentage)?
            }
            PolicyAction::BanNode { node_id, reason } => {
                self.apply_node_ban(node_id, reason)?
            }
        };

        // Store as active policy
        {
            let mut policies = self.active_policies.write().unwrap();
            policies.insert(proposal.id.clone(), action.clone());
        }

        // Audit log
        self.audit_logger.log_event(
            "policy_enforced",
            &format!("proposal={}, action={:?}", proposal.id, action),
        );

        log::info!("⚖️  Policy enforced: {} - {}", proposal.id, result.message);
        Ok(result)
    }

    /// Parse policy action from proposal
    fn parse_policy_action(&self, proposal: &Proposal) -> Result<PolicyAction, String> {
        match proposal.proposal_type {
            ProposalType::ACLModification => {
                let user_id = proposal.metadata.get("user_id")
                    .ok_or("Missing user_id in metadata")?;
                let shard_id = proposal.metadata.get("shard_id")
                    .ok_or("Missing shard_id in metadata")?;
                let permissions: Vec<String> = proposal.metadata.get("permissions")
                    .ok_or("Missing permissions in metadata")?
                    .split(',')
                    .map(|s| s.to_string())
                    .collect();

                Ok(PolicyAction::UpdateACL {
                    user_id: user_id.clone(),
                    shard_id: shard_id.clone(),
                    permissions,
                })
            }
            ProposalType::NetworkUpgrade => {
                let version = proposal.metadata.get("version")
                    .ok_or("Missing version in metadata")?;
                Ok(PolicyAction::UpgradeProtocol { version: version.clone() })
            }
            _ => Err(format!("Unsupported proposal type: {:?}", proposal.proposal_type)),
        }
    }

    /// Apply ACL update
    fn apply_acl_update(
        &self,
        user_id: &str,
        shard_id: &str,
        permissions: &[String],
    ) -> Result<EnforcementResult, String> {
        // TODO: Integrate with actual ACL manager
        log::info!("📋 ACL updated: user={}, shard={}, perms={:?}", 
            user_id, shard_id, permissions);

        Ok(EnforcementResult {
            success: true,
            message: format!("ACL updated for {} on {}", user_id, shard_id),
            timestamp: chrono::Utc::now().timestamp(),
        })
    }

    /// Apply network rule modification
    fn apply_network_rule(&self, rule_id: &str, action: &str) -> Result<EnforcementResult, String> {
        log::info!("🌐 Network rule modified: rule={}, action={}", rule_id, action);

        Ok(EnforcementResult {
            success: true,
            message: format!("Network rule {} {}", rule_id, action),
            timestamp: chrono::Utc::now().timestamp(),
        })
    }

    /// Apply protocol upgrade
    fn apply_protocol_upgrade(&self, version: &str) -> Result<EnforcementResult, String> {
        log::info!("🚀 Protocol upgrade initiated: version={}", version);

        Ok(EnforcementResult {
            success: true,
            message: format!("Protocol upgraded to {}", version),
            timestamp: chrono::Utc::now().timestamp(),
        })
    }

    /// Apply quorum percentage change
    fn apply_quorum_change(&self, new_percentage: f64) -> Result<EnforcementResult, String> {
        log::info!("📊 Quorum changed: {}%", new_percentage);

        Ok(EnforcementResult {
            success: true,
            message: format!("Quorum set to {}%", new_percentage),
            timestamp: chrono::Utc::now().timestamp(),
        })
    }

    /// Apply node ban
    fn apply_node_ban(&self, node_id: &str, reason: &str) -> Result<EnforcementResult, String> {
        log::warn!("🚫 Node banned: id={}, reason={}", node_id, reason);

        Ok(EnforcementResult {
            success: true,
            message: format!("Node {} banned: {}", node_id, reason),
            timestamp: chrono::Utc::now().timestamp(),
        })
    }

    /// Get all active policies
    pub fn get_active_policies(&self) -> HashMap<String, PolicyAction> {
        let policies = self.active_policies.read().unwrap();
        policies.clone()
    }
}
```

## **6. `audit_log.rs` - Cryptographic Audit Trail**

```rust
//! Cryptographic append-only audit log for AuraFS governance
//! f0rg3d in l0v3 by Ross Edwards & Aurphyx

use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use serde::{Serialize, Deserialize};
use sha3::{Digest, Sha3_256};
use chrono::Utc;

/// Audit log entry
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct AuditEntry {
    pub id: u64,
    pub event_type: String,
    pub description: String,
    pub timestamp: i64,
    pub hash: String,
    pub previous_hash: String,
}

/// Append-only audit logger
pub struct AuditLogger {
    entries: Arc<Mutex<Vec<AuditEntry>>>,
}

impl AuditLogger {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            entries: Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new())),
        }
    }

    /// Log an event (append-only, immutable)
    pub fn log_event(&self, event_type: &str, description: &str) {
        let mut entries = self.entries.lock().unwrap();
        
        let id = entries.len() as u64;
        let timestamp = Utc::now().timestamp();
        let previous_hash = entries.last()
            .map(|e| e.hash.clone())
            .unwrap_or_else(|| "0".repeat(64));

        let hash = self.compute_hash(id, event_type, description, timestamp, &previous_hash);

        let entry = AuditEntry {
            id,
            event_type: event_type.to_string(),
            description: description.to_string(),
            timestamp,
            hash,
            previous_hash,
        };

        entries.push(entry);
        log::debug!("📜 Audit log: {} - {}", event_type, description);
    }

    /// Compute cryptographic hash for entry
    fn compute_hash(
        &self,
        id: u64,
        event_type: &str,
        description: &str,
        timestamp: i64,
        previous_hash: &str,
    ) -> String {
        let data = format!("{}:{}:{}:{}:{}", id, event_type, description, timestamp, previous_hash);
        let mut hasher = Sha3_256::new();
        hasher.update(data.as_bytes());
        format!("{:x}", hasher.finalize())
    }

    /// Verify audit log integrity (check hash chain)
    pub fn verify_integrity(&self) -> Result<bool, String> {
        let entries = self.entries.lock().unwrap();
        
        for (i, entry) in entries.iter().enumerate() {
            let expected_hash = self.compute_hash(
                entry.id,
                &entry.event_type,
                &entry.description,
                entry.timestamp,
                &entry.previous_hash,
            );

            if entry.hash != expected_hash {
                return Err(format!("Hash mismatch at entry {}: expected={}, got={}", 
                    i, expected_hash, entry.hash));
            }

            if i > 0 {
                let prev_entry = &entries[i - 1];
                if entry.previous_hash != prev_entry.hash {
                    return Err(format!("Chain broken at entry {}", i));
                }
            }
        }

        Ok(true)
    }

    /// Get all audit entries
    pub fn get_entries(&self) -> Vec<AuditEntry> {
        let entries = self.entries.lock().unwrap();
        entries.clone()
    }

    /// Export audit log to JSON
    pub fn export_json(&self) -> Result<String, String> {
        let entries = self.get_entries();
        serde_json::to_string_pretty(&entries)
            .map_err(|e| format!("Export failed: {}", e))
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_audit_log_integrity() {
        let logger = AuditLogger::new();

        logger.log_event("test_event_1", "First event");
        logger.log_event("test_event_2", "Second event");
        logger.log_event("test_event_3", "Third event");

        assert!(logger.verify_integrity().is_ok());

        let entries = logger.get_entries();
        assert_eq!(entries.len(), 3);
        assert_eq!(entries[1].previous_hash, entries[0].hash);
    }
}
```

## **7. `models.rs` - Core Data Structures**

```rust
//! Core data models for AuraFS governance system
//! f0rg3d in l0v3 by Ross Edwards & Aurphyx

use std::collections::HashMap;
use serde::{Serialize, Deserialize};

/// Proposal status in the governance lifecycle
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum ProposalStatus {
    Draft,
    Active,
    Approved,
    Rejected,
    Cancelled,
    Executed,
}

/// Types of governance proposals
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum ProposalType {
    ACLModification,
    NetworkUpgrade,
    ParameterChange,
    NodeBan,
    TreasuryAllocation,
    ProtocolUpgrade,
}

/// Governance proposal
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Proposal {
    pub id: String,
    pub title: String,
    pub description: String,
    pub creator_bliss_id: String,
    pub proposal_type: ProposalType,
    pub status: ProposalStatus,
    pub created_at: i64,
    pub updated_at: i64,
    pub voting_start: Option<i64>,
    pub voting_end: Option<i64>,
    pub metadata: HashMap<String, String>,
}

/// Soul coherence metrics for vote weighting
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct SoulCoherence {
    pub bliss_id: String,
    pub hrv_coherence: f64,          // Heart rate variability (0-1)
    pub participation_rate: f64,      // Governance participation (0-1)
    pub ethical_alignment: f64,       // Ethical score (0-1)
    pub overall_coherence: f64,       // Aggregate (0-1)
    pub last_updated: i64,
}

impl SoulCoherence {
    pub fn compute_vote_weight(&self) -> f64 {
        // Base weight 1.0 + up to 0.5 bonus from coherence
        1.0 + (0.5 * self.overall_coherence)
    }
}

/// Network node information
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct NetworkNode {
    pub node_id: String,
    pub address: String,
    pub role: String,  // "validator", "observer", "leader"
    pub reputation_score: f64,
    pub last_seen: i64,
    pub active: bool,
}

/// ACL permission entry
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ACLEntry {
    pub user_id: String,
    pub resource_id: String,
    pub permissions: Vec<String>,  // ["read", "write", "admin"]
    pub granted_at: i64,
    pub expires_at: Option<i64>,
}
```

## **8. `soulsync_engine.rs` - Soul Coherence Engine**

```rust
//! SoulSync coherence scoring engine for vote weighting
//! f0rg3d in l0v3 by Ross Edwards & Aurphyx

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::{Arc, RwLock};
use serde::{Serialize, Deserialize};
use chrono::Utc;

use crate::models::SoulCoherence;
use crate::audit_log::AuditLogger;

/// SoulSync engine for computing soul coherence scores
pub struct SoulSyncEngine {
    coherence_cache: Arc<RwLock<HashMap<String, SoulCoherence>>>,
    audit_logger: Arc<AuditLogger>,
}

impl SoulSyncEngine {
    pub fn new(audit_logger: Arc<AuditLogger>) -> Self {
        Self {
            coherence_cache: Arc::new(RwLock::new(HashMap::new())),
            audit_logger,
        }
    }

    /// Compute vote weight for a BlissID based on soul coherence
    pub async fn compute_vote_weight(&self, bliss_id: &str) -> Result<f64, String> {
        let coherence = self.get_or_compute_coherence(bliss_id).await?;
        Ok(coherence.compute_vote_weight())
    }

    /// Get or compute coherence for a BlissID
    async fn get_or_compute_coherence(&self, bliss_id: &str) -> Result<SoulCoherence, String> {
        // Check cache first
        {
            let cache = self.coherence_cache.read().unwrap();
            if let Some(coherence) = cache.get(bliss_id) {
                // Check if cache is fresh (within 1 hour)
                let now = Utc::now().timestamp();
                if now - coherence.last_updated < 3600 {
                    return Ok(coherence.clone());
                }
            }
        }

        // Compute fresh coherence
        let coherence = self.compute_coherence(bliss_id).await?;

        // Update cache
        {
            let mut cache = self.coherence_cache.write().unwrap();
            cache.insert(bliss_id.to_string(), coherence.clone());
        }

        self.audit_logger.log_event(
            "coherence_computed",
            &format!("bliss_id={}, coherence={:.3}", bliss_id, coherence.overall_coherence),
        );

        Ok(coherence)
    }

    /// Compute coherence metrics for a soul
    async fn compute_coherence(&self, bliss_id: &str) -> Result<SoulCoherence, String> {
        // TODO: Integrate with actual data sources:
        // - HRV from wearable devices
        // - Participation from governance history
        // - Ethical alignment from behavior analysis

        // For now, return default coherence
        let hrv_coherence = 0.7;  // Placeholder
        let participation_rate = 0.5;  // Placeholder
        let ethical_alignment = 0.8;  // Placeholder

        let overall = (hrv_coherence + participation_rate + ethical_alignment) / 3.0;

        Ok(SoulCoherence {
            bliss_id: bliss_id.to_string(),
            hrv_coherence,
            participation_rate,
            ethical_alignment,
            overall_coherence: overall,
            last_updated: Utc::now().timestamp(),
        })
    }

    /// Update coherence metric manually (for testing/admin)
    pub fn update_coherence(
        &self,
        bliss_id: String,
        hrv: f64,
        participation: f64,
        ethical: f64,
    ) -> SoulCoherence {
        let overall = (hrv + participation + ethical) / 3.0;
        let coherence = SoulCoherence {
            bliss_id: bliss_id.clone(),
            hrv_coherence: hrv,
            participation_rate: participation,
            ethical_alignment: ethical,
            overall_coherence: overall,
            last_updated: Utc::now().timestamp(),
        };

        let mut cache = self.coherence_cache.write().unwrap();
        cache.insert(bliss_id, coherence.clone());

        coherence
    }

    /// Get coherence for a BlissID (from cache)
    pub fn get_coherence(&self, bliss_id: &str) -> Option<SoulCoherence> {
        let cache = self.coherence_cache.read().unwrap();
        cache.get(bliss_id).cloned()
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn test_vote_weight_computation() {
        let audit_logger = Arc::new(AuditLogger::new());
        let engine = SoulSyncEngine::new(audit_logger);

        let weight = engine.compute_vote_weight("bliss:test").await.unwrap();
        
        // Base weight 1.0 + coherence bonus
        assert!(weight >= 1.0 && weight <= 1.5);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_manual_coherence_update() {
        let audit_logger = Arc::new(AuditLogger::new());
        let engine = SoulSyncEngine::new(audit_logger);

        let coherence = engine.update_coherence(
            "bliss:manual".to_string(),
            0.9,  // high HRV
            0.8,  // good participation
            0.95, // strong ethics
        );

        assert_eq!(coherence.overall_coherence, (0.9 + 0.8 + 0.95) / 3.0);
        assert!(coherence.compute_vote_weight() > 1.4);
    }
}
```

## **9. `blissid_manager.rs` - BlissID Lifecycle Management**

```rust
//! BlissID lifecycle and registration management
//! f0rg3d in l0v3 by Ross Edwards & Aurphyx

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::{Arc, RwLock};
use serde::{Serialize, Deserialize};
use chrono::Utc;
use sha3::{Digest, Sha3_256};

use crate::identity_verifier::{BlissID, SoulProof};
use crate::audit_log::AuditLogger;

/// BlissID manager for registration and lifecycle
pub struct BlissIDManager {
    registry: Arc<RwLock<HashMap<String, BlissID>>>,
    soul_hash_index: Arc<RwLock<HashMap<String, String>>>,  // soul_hash -> bliss_id
    audit_logger: Arc<AuditLogger>,
}

impl BlissIDManager {
    pub fn new(audit_logger: Arc<AuditLogger>) -> Self {
        Self {
            registry: Arc::new(RwLock::new(HashMap::new())),
            soul_hash_index: Arc::new(RwLock::new(HashMap::new())),
            audit_logger,
        }
    }

    /// Register a new BlissID (after ID.me + ABIS verification)
    pub fn register_bliss_id(
        &self,
        did: String,
        soul_hash: String,
        soul_proof: SoulProof,
    ) -> Result<BlissID, String> {
        // Check for duplicate soul_hash (ONE SOUL = ONE ACCOUNT)
        {
            let index = self.soul_hash_index.read().unwrap();
            if let Some(existing_id) = index.get(&soul_hash) {
                return Err(format!("Soul already registered with BlissID: {}", existing_id));
            }
        }

        // Generate new BlissID
        let bliss_id = format!("bliss:{}", uuid::Uuid::new_v4());
        let now = Utc::now().timestamp();

        let bliss = BlissID {
            id: bliss_id.clone(),
            did,
            soul_hash: soul_hash.clone(),
            created_at: now,
            active: true,
            soul_proof,
        };

        // Store in registry
        {
            let mut registry = self.registry.write().unwrap();
            registry.insert(bliss_id.clone(), bliss.clone());

            let mut index = self.soul_hash_index.write().unwrap();
            index.insert(soul_hash, bliss_id.clone());
        }

        self.audit_logger.log_event(
            "blissid_registered",
            &format!("id={}, did={}", bliss_id, bliss.did),
        );

        log::info!("✨ BlissID registered: {}", bliss_id);
        Ok(bliss)
    }

    /// Verify BlissID is valid and active
    pub fn verify_bliss_id(&self, bliss_id: &str) -> bool {
        let registry = self.registry.read().unwrap();
        registry.get(bliss_id)
            .map(|id| id.active)
            .unwrap_or(false)
    }

    /// Get BlissID by ID
    pub fn get_bliss_id(&self, bliss_id: &str) -> Option<BlissID> {
        let registry = self.registry.read().unwrap();
        registry.get(bliss_id).cloned()
    }

    /// Deactivate a BlissID (for security violations)
    pub fn deactivate_bliss_id(&self, bliss_id: &str, reason: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        let mut registry = self.registry.write().unwrap();
        
        let bliss = registry.get_mut(bliss_id)
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("BlissID not found: {}", bliss_id))?;

        bliss.active = false;

        self.audit_logger.log_event(
            "blissid_deactivated",
            &format!("id={}, reason={}", bliss_id, reason),
        );

        log::warn!("🚫 BlissID deactivated: {} (reason: {})", bliss_id, reason);
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Reactivate a BlissID
    pub fn reactivate_bliss_id(&self, bliss_id: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        let mut registry = self.registry.write().unwrap();
        
        let bliss = registry.get_mut(bliss_id)
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("BlissID not found: {}", bliss_id))?;

        bliss.active = true;

        self.audit_logger.log_event(
            "blissid_reactivated",
            &format!("id={}", bliss_id),
        );

        log::info!("✅ BlissID reactivated: {}", bliss_id);
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Check if soul_hash already exists
    pub fn soul_exists(&self, soul_hash: &str) -> bool {
        let index = self.soul_hash_index.read().unwrap();
        index.contains_key(soul_hash)
    }

    /// Get total registered BlissIDs
    pub fn get_total_count(&self) -> usize {
        let registry = self.registry.read().unwrap();
        registry.len()
    }

    /// Get active BlissIDs count
    pub fn get_active_count(&self) -> usize {
        let registry = self.registry.read().unwrap();
        registry.values().filter(|b| b.active).count()
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_blissid_registration() {
        let audit_logger = Arc::new(AuditLogger::new());
        let manager = BlissIDManager::new(audit_logger);

        let soul_proof = SoulProof {
            commitment: "test_commit".to_string(),
            proof_type: "zk-SNARK-unique-human".to_string(),
            verifiable: true,
        };

        let bliss = manager.register_bliss_id(
            "did:test:123".to_string(),
            "soul_hash_123".to_string(),
            soul_proof,
        ).unwrap();

        assert!(manager.verify_bliss_id(&bliss.id));
        assert_eq!(manager.get_total_count(), 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_duplicate_soul_prevention() {
        let audit_logger = Arc::new(AuditLogger::new());
        let manager = BlissIDManager::new(audit_logger);

        let soul_proof = SoulProof {
            commitment: "test".to_string(),
            proof_type: "zk-SNARK-unique-human".to_string(),
            verifiable: true,
        };

        // Register first soul
        manager.register_bliss_id(
            "did:1".to_string(),
            "duplicate_hash".to_string(),
            soul_proof.clone(),
        ).unwrap();

        // Try to register same soul again
        let result = manager.register_bliss_id(
            "did:2".to_string(),
            "duplicate_hash".to_string(),
            soul_proof,
        );

        assert!(result.is_err());
        assert!(result.unwrap_err().contains("already registered"));
    }
}
```

## **10. `lib.rs` - Module Root \& Public API**

```rust
//! AuraFS Governance Module - Root
//! f0rg3d in l0v3 by Ross Edwards & Aurphyx
//!
//! Complete governance system with BlissID identity, soul-weighted voting,
//! distributed consensus, and policy enforcement.

pub mod models;
pub mod identity_verifier;
pub mod blissid_manager;
pub mod soulsync_engine;
pub mod voting_engine;
pub mod proposal_manager;
pub mod consensus_integration;
pub mod policy_enforcer;
pub mod audit_log;

// Re-export key types for convenience
pub use models::*;
pub use identity_verifier::{IdentityVerifier, BlissID, SoulProof};
pub use blissid_manager::BlissIDManager;
pub use soulsync_engine::SoulSyncEngine;
pub use voting_engine::{VotingEngine, Vote, VoteOption, VoteTally};
pub use proposal_manager::{ProposalManager, ProposalConfig};
pub use consensus_integration::{ConsensusIntegration, GovernanceTransaction, LedgerClient};
pub use policy_enforcer::{PolicyEnforcer, PolicyAction};
pub use audit_log::{AuditLogger, AuditEntry};

use std::sync::Arc;

/// Complete governance system builder
pub struct GovernanceSystem {
    pub identity_verifier: Arc<IdentityVerifier>,
    pub blissid_manager: Arc<BlissIDManager>,
    pub soulsync_engine: Arc<SoulSyncEngine>,
    pub audit_logger: Arc<AuditLogger>,
    pub consensus: Arc<ConsensusIntegration>,
    pub voting_engine: Arc<VotingEngine>,
    pub proposal_manager: Arc<ProposalManager>,
    pub policy_enforcer: Arc<PolicyEnforcer>,
}

impl GovernanceSystem {
    /// Initialize complete governance system
    pub fn new(
        node_id: String,
        validators: Vec<String>,
        ledger_client: Arc<dyn LedgerClient + Send + Sync>,
        proposal_config: Option<ProposalConfig>,
    ) -> Self {
        let audit_logger = Arc::new(AuditLogger::new());
        let identity_verifier = Arc::new(IdentityVerifier::new());
        let blissid_manager = Arc::new(BlissIDManager::new(audit_logger.clone()));
        let soulsync_engine = Arc::new(SoulSyncEngine::new(audit_logger.clone()));
        
        let consensus = Arc::new(ConsensusIntegration::new(
            node_id,
            validators,
            ledger_client,
        ));
        
        let voting_engine = Arc::new(VotingEngine::new(
            identity_verifier.clone(),
            consensus.clone(),
        ));
        
        let proposal_manager = Arc::new(ProposalManager::new(
            voting_engine.clone(),
            audit_logger.clone(),
            proposal_config,
        ));
        
        let policy_enforcer = Arc::new(PolicyEnforcer::new(audit_logger.clone()));

        Self {
            identity_verifier,
            blissid_manager,
            soulsync_engine,
            audit_logger,
            consensus,
            voting_engine,
            proposal_manager,
            policy_enforcer,
        }
    }

    /// Verify audit log integrity
    pub fn verify_system_integrity(&self) -> Result<bool, String> {
        self.audit_logger.verify_integrity()
    }

    /// Export complete system state to JSON
    pub fn export_state(&self) -> Result<String, String> {
        self.audit_logger.export_json()
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use consensus_integration::MockLedgerClient;

    #[test]
    fn test_governance_system_initialization() {
        let ledger = Arc::new(MockLedgerClient::new());
        let system = GovernanceSystem::new(
            "test_node".to_string(),
            vec!["node1".to_string(), "node2".to_string()],
            ledger,
            None,
        );

        assert!(system.verify_system_integrity().is_ok());
        assert_eq!(system.blissid_manager.get_total_count(), 0);
    }
}
```

## **11. `api.rs` - High-Performance Axum REST API Server**

```rust
//! Production Axum REST API server for AuraFS governance
//! f0rg3d in l0v3 by Ross Edwards & Aurphyx

use axum::{
    extract::{State, Path, Query},
    http::StatusCode,
    response::{IntoResponse, Json},
    routing::{get, post, put},
    Router,
};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use tower_http::cors::CorsLayer;

use crate::{
    GovernanceSystem,
    ProposalType, ProposalStatus,
    VoteOption, SoulProof,
};

/// Application state shared across handlers
#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct AppState {
    pub governance: Arc<GovernanceSystem>,
}

/// API error response
#[derive(Serialize)]
pub struct ErrorResponse {
    pub error: String,
    pub message: String,
}

/// Success response wrapper
#[derive(Serialize)]
pub struct SuccessResponse<T> {
    pub success: bool,
    pub data: T,
}

// ==================== Request/Response Models ====================

#[derive(Deserialize)]
pub struct CreateProposalRequest {
    pub creator_bliss_id: String,
    pub title: String,
    pub description: String,
    pub proposal_type: ProposalType,
    pub voting_period_hours: i64,
    pub metadata: Option<HashMap<String, String>>,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
pub struct CastVoteRequest {
    pub proposal_id: String,
    pub bliss_id: String,
    pub option: VoteOption,
    pub quantum_signature: String,  // Hex-encoded
    pub soul_proof: SoulProof,
    pub metadata: Option<HashMap<String, String>>,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
pub struct RegisterBlissIDRequest {
    pub did: String,
    pub soul_hash: String,
    pub soul_proof: SoulProof,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
pub struct QueryParams {
    pub status: Option<ProposalStatus>,
}

// ==================== API Handlers ====================

/// Health check endpoint
async fn health_check() -> impl IntoResponse {
    Json(serde_json::json!({
        "status": "healthy",
        "service": "aurafs-governance-api",
        "version": "1.0.0"
    }))
}

/// Create a new proposal
async fn create_proposal(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Json(req): Json<CreateProposalRequest>,
) -> Result<Json<SuccessResponse<serde_json::Value>>, (StatusCode, Json<ErrorResponse>)> {
    match state.governance.proposal_manager.create_proposal(
        req.creator_bliss_id,
        req.title,
        req.description,
        req.proposal_type,
        req.voting_period_hours,
        req.metadata,
    ) {
        Ok(proposal) => Ok(Json(SuccessResponse {
            success: true,
            data: serde_json::to_value(proposal).unwrap(),
        })),
        Err(e) => Err((
            StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,
            Json(ErrorResponse {
                error: "proposal_creation_failed".to_string(),
                message: e,
            }),
        )),
    }
}

/// Submit proposal for voting
async fn submit_proposal(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Path(proposal_id): Path<String>,
) -> Result<Json<SuccessResponse<serde_json::Value>>, (StatusCode, Json<ErrorResponse>)> {
    match state.governance.proposal_manager.submit_proposal(&proposal_id) {
        Ok(proposal) => Ok(Json(SuccessResponse {
            success: true,
            data: serde_json::to_value(proposal).unwrap(),
        })),
        Err(e) => Err((
            StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,
            Json(ErrorResponse {
                error: "proposal_submission_failed".to_string(),
                message: e,
            }),
        )),
    }
}

/// Get proposal by ID
async fn get_proposal(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Path(proposal_id): Path<String>,
) -> Result<Json<SuccessResponse<serde_json::Value>>, (StatusCode, Json<ErrorResponse>)> {
    match state.governance.proposal_manager.get_proposal(&proposal_id) {
        Some(proposal) => Ok(Json(SuccessResponse {
            success: true,
            data: serde_json::to_value(proposal).unwrap(),
        })),
        None => Err((
            StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,
            Json(ErrorResponse {
                error: "proposal_not_found".to_string(),
                message: format!("Proposal {} does not exist", proposal_id),
            }),
        )),
    }
}

/// List proposals with optional status filter
async fn list_proposals(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Query(params): Query<QueryParams>,
) -> Json<SuccessResponse<Vec<serde_json::Value>>> {
    let proposals = state.governance.proposal_manager.list_proposals(params.status);
    let data: Vec<serde_json::Value> = proposals
        .into_iter()
        .map(|p| serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap())
        .collect();

    Json(SuccessResponse {
        success: true,
        data,
    })
}

/// Cast a vote
async fn cast_vote(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Json(req): Json<CastVoteRequest>,
) -> Result<Json<SuccessResponse<serde_json::Value>>, (StatusCode, Json<ErrorResponse>)> {
    // Decode hex signature
    let signature = hex::decode(&req.quantum_signature)
        .map_err(|e| (
            StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,
            Json(ErrorResponse {
                error: "invalid_signature_encoding".to_string(),
                message: format!("Failed to decode hex signature: {}", e),
            }),
        ))?;

    match state.governance.voting_engine.cast_vote(
        req.proposal_id,
        req.bliss_id,
        req.option,
        signature,
        req.soul_proof,
        req.metadata,
    ).await {
        Ok(vote) => Ok(Json(SuccessResponse {
            success: true,
            data: serde_json::to_value(vote).unwrap(),
        })),
        Err(e) => Err((
            StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,
            Json(ErrorResponse {
                error: "vote_casting_failed".to_string(),
                message: e,
            }),
        )),
    }
}

/// Get vote tally for a proposal
async fn get_vote_tally(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Path(proposal_id): Path<String>,
) -> Json<SuccessResponse<serde_json::Value>> {
    let tally = state.governance.voting_engine.tally_votes(&proposal_id);
    
    Json(SuccessResponse {
        success: true,
        data: serde_json::to_value(tally).unwrap(),
    })
}

/// Register a new BlissID
async fn register_blissid(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Json(req): Json<RegisterBlissIDRequest>,
) -> Result<Json<SuccessResponse<serde_json::Value>>, (StatusCode, Json<ErrorResponse>)> {
    match state.governance.blissid_manager.register_bliss_id(
        req.did,
        req.soul_hash,
        req.soul_proof,
    ) {
        Ok(bliss_id) => Ok(Json(SuccessResponse {
            success: true,
            data: serde_json::to_value(bliss_id).unwrap(),
        })),
        Err(e) => Err((
            StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,
            Json(ErrorResponse {
                error: "blissid_registration_failed".to_string(),
                message: e,
            }),
        )),
    }
}

/// Verify BlissID
async fn verify_blissid(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Path(bliss_id): Path<String>,
) -> Json<SuccessResponse<serde_json::Value>> {
    let is_valid = state.governance.blissid_manager.verify_bliss_id(&bliss_id);
    
    Json(SuccessResponse {
        success: true,
        data: serde_json::json!({
            "bliss_id": bliss_id,
            "valid": is_valid,
        }),
    })
}

/// Get audit log
async fn get_audit_log(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
) -> Json<SuccessResponse<Vec<serde_json::Value>>> {
    let entries = state.governance.audit_logger.get_entries();
    let data: Vec<serde_json::Value> = entries
        .into_iter()
        .map(|e| serde_json::to_value(e).unwrap())
        .collect();

    Json(SuccessResponse {
        success: true,
        data,
    })
}

/// Verify system integrity
async fn verify_integrity(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
) -> Result<Json<SuccessResponse<serde_json::Value>>, (StatusCode, Json<ErrorResponse>)> {
    match state.governance.verify_system_integrity() {
        Ok(valid) => Ok(Json(SuccessResponse {
            success: true,
            data: serde_json::json!({
                "integrity_valid": valid,
                "message": "Audit log integrity verified"
            }),
        })),
        Err(e) => Err((
            StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
            Json(ErrorResponse {
                error: "integrity_check_failed".to_string(),
                message: e,
            }),
        )),
    }
}

/// Finalize a proposal
async fn finalize_proposal(
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Path(proposal_id): Path<String>,
) -> Result<Json<SuccessResponse<serde_json::Value>>, (StatusCode, Json<ErrorResponse>)> {
    match state.governance.proposal_manager.finalize_proposal(&proposal_id) {
        Ok(proposal) => Ok(Json(SuccessResponse {
            success: true,
            data: serde_json::to_value(proposal).unwrap(),
        })),
        Err(e) => Err((
            StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,
            Json(ErrorResponse {
                error: "finalization_failed".to_string(),
                message: e,
            }),
        )),
    }
}

// ==================== Router Setup ====================

/// Build the Axum router with all routes
pub fn build_router(state: AppState) -> Router {
    Router::new()
        // Health check
        .route("/health", get(health_check))
        
        // Proposals
        .route("/api/v1/proposals", post(create_proposal))
        .route("/api/v1/proposals", get(list_proposals))
        .route("/api/v1/proposals/:id", get(get_proposal))
        .route("/api/v1/proposals/:id/submit", post(submit_proposal))
        .route("/api/v1/proposals/:id/finalize", post(finalize_proposal))
        
        // Voting
        .route("/api/v1/votes", post(cast_vote))
        .route("/api/v1/proposals/:id/tally", get(get_vote_tally))
        
        // BlissID
        .route("/api/v1/blissid/register", post(register_blissid))
        .route("/api/v1/blissid/:id/verify", get(verify_blissid))
        
        // Audit
        .route("/api/v1/audit/log", get(get_audit_log))
        .route("/api/v1/audit/verify", get(verify_integrity))
        
        .with_state(state)
        .layer(CorsLayer::permissive())
}

/// Start the API server
pub async fn start_server(governance: Arc<GovernanceSystem>, port: u16) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let state = AppState { governance };
    let app = build_router(state);

    let addr = format!("0.0.0.0:{}", port);
    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind(&addr).await?;
    
    log::info!("🚀 AuraFS Governance API listening on {}", addr);
    
    axum::serve(listener, app).await?;
    
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use axum::http::Request;
    use tower::ServiceExt;
    use crate::consensus_integration::MockLedgerClient;

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn test_health_check() {
        let ledger = Arc::new(MockLedgerClient::new());
        let governance = Arc::new(GovernanceSystem::new(
            "test_node".to_string(),
            vec!["node1".to_string()],
            ledger,
            None,
        ));
        
        let app = build_router(AppState { governance });
        
        let response = app
            .oneshot(Request::builder().uri("/health").body(axum::body::Body::empty()).unwrap())
            .await
            .unwrap();

        assert_eq!(response.status(), StatusCode::OK);
    }
}
```

## **12. `server.rs` - Complete Server Binary with CLI**

```rust
//! AuraFS Governance API Server Binary
//! f0rg3d in l0v3 by Ross Edwards & Aurphyx

use std::sync::Arc;
use clap::Parser;
use env_logger::Env;

use afs_governance::{
    GovernanceSystem,
    consensus_integration::MockLedgerClient,
    api::start_server,
};

/// CLI arguments
#[derive(Parser, Debug)]
#[command(name = "aurafs-governance-server")]
#[command(about = "AuraFS Governance API Server", long_about = None)]
struct Args {
    /// Port to listen on
    #[arg(short, long, default_value_t = 8080)]
    port: u16,

    /// Node ID for consensus
    #[arg(short, long, default_value = "node1")]
    node_id: String,

    /// Validator nodes (comma-separated)
    #[arg(short, long, default_value = "node1,node2,node3")]
    validators: String,

    /// Log level (trace, debug, info, warn, error)
    #[arg(short, long, default_value = "info")]
    log_level: String,
}

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let args = Args::parse();

    // Initialize logger
    env_logger::Builder::from_env(Env::default().default_filter_or(&args.log_level))
        .init();

    log::info!("🚀 Starting AuraFS Governance Server...");
    log::info!("Node ID: {}", args.node_id);
    log::info!("Port: {}", args.port);

    // Parse validators
    let validators: Vec<String> = args.validators
        .split(',')
        .map(|s| s.trim().to_string())
        .collect();
    
    log::info!("Validators: {:?}", validators);

    // Initialize governance system
    let ledger = Arc::new(MockLedgerClient::new());
    let governance = Arc::new(GovernanceSystem::new(
        args.node_id,
        validators,
        ledger,
        None,
    ));

    log::info!("✅ Governance system initialized");

    // Start API server
    start_server(governance, args.port).await?;

    Ok(())
}
```

***

## **📡 API Endpoints**

### **Proposals**

- `POST /api/v1/proposals` - Create proposal
- `GET /api/v1/proposals` - List proposals (optional `?status=Active`)
- `GET /api/v1/proposals/:id` - Get proposal
- `POST /api/v1/proposals/:id/submit` - Submit for voting
- `POST /api/v1/proposals/:id/finalize` - Finalize voting


### **Voting**

- `POST /api/v1/votes` - Cast vote
- `GET /api/v1/proposals/:id/tally` - Get vote tally


### **BlissID**

- `POST /api/v1/blissid/register` - Register BlissID
- `GET /api/v1/blissid/:id/verify` - Verify BlissID


### **Audit**

- `GET /api/v1/audit/log` - Get audit log
- `GET /api/v1/audit/verify` - Verify integrity


### **Health**

- `GET /health` - Health check

```rust
//! AuraFS Governance System - Main Entry Point & Interactive CLI
//! f0rg3d in l0v3 by Ross Edwards & Aurphyx
//!
//! Complete governance demonstration with:
//! - Interactive CLI for all governance operations
//! - Full workflow demos (proposal creation → voting → finalization)
//! - System diagnostics and integrity checks
//! - BlissID registration and management
//! - Real-time vote tallying and statistics

use std::sync::Arc;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use clap::{Parser, Subcommand};
use env_logger::Env;
use chrono::{Utc, DateTime, Local};

mod models;
mod identity_verifier;
mod blissid_manager;
mod soulsync_engine;
mod voting_engine;
mod proposal_manager;
mod consensus_integration;
mod policy_enforcer;
mod audit_log;
mod api;

use models::*;
use identity_verifier::*;
use blissid_manager::*;
use soulsync_engine::*;
use voting_engine::*;
use proposal_manager::*;
use consensus_integration::*;
use policy_enforcer::*;
use audit_log::*;

use pqcrypto_dilithium::dilithium5;
use pqcrypto_traits::sign::*;

/// Complete governance system
pub struct GovernanceSystem {
    pub identity_verifier: Arc<IdentityVerifier>,
    pub blissid_manager: Arc<BlissIDManager>,
    pub soulsync_engine: Arc<SoulSyncEngine>,
    pub audit_logger: Arc<AuditLogger>,
    pub consensus: Arc<ConsensusIntegration>,
    pub voting_engine: Arc<VotingEngine>,
    pub proposal_manager: Arc<ProposalManager>,
    pub policy_enforcer: Arc<PolicyEnforcer>,
}

impl GovernanceSystem {
    pub fn new(
        node_id: String,
        validators: Vec<String>,
        ledger_client: Arc<dyn LedgerClient + Send + Sync>,
        proposal_config: Option<ProposalConfig>,
    ) -> Self {
        let audit_logger = Arc::new(AuditLogger::new());
        let identity_verifier = Arc::new(IdentityVerifier::new());
        let blissid_manager = Arc::new(BlissIDManager::new(audit_logger.clone()));
        let soulsync_engine = Arc::new(SoulSyncEngine::new(audit_logger.clone()));
        
        let consensus = Arc::new(ConsensusIntegration::new(
            node_id,
            validators,
            ledger_client,
        ));
        
        let voting_engine = Arc::new(VotingEngine::new(
            identity_verifier.clone(),
            consensus.clone(),
        ));
        
        let proposal_manager = Arc::new(ProposalManager::new(
            voting_engine.clone(),
            audit_logger.clone(),
            proposal_config,
        ));
        
        let policy_enforcer = Arc::new(PolicyEnforcer::new(audit_logger.clone()));

        Self {
            identity_verifier,
            blissid_manager,
            soulsync_engine,
            audit_logger,
            consensus,
            voting_engine,
            proposal_manager,
            policy_enforcer,
        }
    }

    pub fn verify_system_integrity(&self) -> Result<bool, String> {
        self.audit_logger.verify_integrity()
    }
}

// ==================== CLI Arguments ====================

#[derive(Parser)]
#[command(name = "aurafs-governance")]
#[command(author = "Ross Edwards & Aurphyx")]
#[command(version = "1.0.0")]
#[command(about = "AuraFS Governance System - One Soul, One Vote", long_about = None)]
struct Cli {
    #[command(subcommand)]
    command: Commands,

    /// Log level
    #[arg(short, long, default_value = "info", global = true)]
    log_level: String,
}

#[derive(Subcommand)]
enum Commands {
    /// Run complete interactive demo workflow
    Demo {
        /// Number of voters to simulate
        #[arg(short, long, default_value_t = 5)]
        voters: usize,
    },
    
    /// Start the API server
    Serve {
        /// Port to listen on
        #[arg(short, long, default_value_t = 8080)]
        port: u16,
    },
    
    /// Register a new BlissID
    RegisterBlissID {
        /// Decentralized Identifier
        #[arg(short, long)]
        did: String,
    },
    
    /// Create a new proposal
    CreateProposal {
        /// Creator BlissID
        #[arg(short, long)]
        creator: String,
        
        /// Proposal title
        #[arg(short, long)]
        title: String,
        
        /// Voting period in hours
        #[arg(short = 'p', long, default_value_t = 48)]
        voting_period: i64,
    },
    
    /// Cast a vote
    Vote {
        /// Proposal ID
        #[arg(short, long)]
        proposal_id: String,
        
        /// Voter BlissID
        #[arg(short, long)]
        bliss_id: String,
        
        /// Vote option (yes/no/abstain)
        #[arg(short = 'o', long)]
        option: String,
    },
    
    /// Show system diagnostics
    Diagnostics,
    
    /// Verify system integrity
    Verify,
    
    /// Export audit log
    Export {
        /// Output file path
        #[arg(short, long, default_value = "audit_log.json")]
        output: String,
    },
}

// ==================== Main Entry Point ====================

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let cli = Cli::parse();

    // Initialize logger
    env_logger::Builder::from_env(Env::default().default_filter_or(&cli.log_level))
        .format_timestamp_millis()
        .init();

    // Print banner
    print_banner();

    // Initialize governance system
    let ledger = Arc::new(MockLedgerClient::new());
    let governance = Arc::new(GovernanceSystem::new(
        "main_node".to_string(),
        vec!["node1".to_string(), "node2".to_string(), "node3".to_string()],
        ledger,
        None,
    ));

    // Execute command
    match cli.command {
        Commands::Demo { voters } => {
            run_interactive_demo(governance, voters).await?;
        }
        Commands::Serve { port } => {
            api::start_server(governance, port).await?;
        }
        Commands::RegisterBlissID { did } => {
            register_blissid_interactive(governance, did).await?;
        }
        Commands::CreateProposal { creator, title, voting_period } => {
            create_proposal_interactive(governance, creator, title, voting_period).await?;
        }
        Commands::Vote { proposal_id, bliss_id, option } => {
            cast_vote_interactive(governance, proposal_id, bliss_id, option).await?;
        }
        Commands::Diagnostics => {
            show_diagnostics(governance).await?;
        }
        Commands::Verify => {
            verify_integrity(governance).await?;
        }
        Commands::Export { output } => {
            export_audit_log(governance, output).await?;
        }
    }

    Ok(())
}

// ==================== Interactive Demo ====================

async fn run_interactive_demo(
    governance: Arc<GovernanceSystem>,
    voter_count: usize,
) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    println!("\n{}", "=".repeat(80));
    println!("🎭 AURAFS GOVERNANCE INTERACTIVE DEMO");
    println!("{}\n", "=".repeat(80));

    // Step 1: Register voters
    println!("📝 STEP 1: Registering {} BlissID voters...\n", voter_count);
    let mut voters = Vec::new();
    
    for i in 1..=voter_count {
        let (pk, sk) = dilithium5::keypair();
        let bliss_id = format!("bliss:voter{}", i);
        let did = format!("did:aurafs:voter{}", i);
        let soul_hash = format!("soul_hash_{}", i);
        
        let soul_proof = SoulProof {
            commitment: format!("commitment_{}", i),
            proof_type: "zk-SNARK-unique-human".to_string(),
            verifiable: true,
        };

        governance.identity_verifier.register_bliss_id(
            BlissID {
                id: bliss_id.clone(),
                did: did.clone(),
                soul_hash: soul_hash.clone(),
                created_at: Utc::now().timestamp(),
                active: true,
                soul_proof: soul_proof.clone(),
            },
            pk,
        )?;

        governance.blissid_manager.register_bliss_id(
            did,
            soul_hash,
            soul_proof,
        )?;

        voters.push((bliss_id, sk));
        println!("  ✅ Registered: bliss:voter{}", i);
    }

    println!("\n✨ {} BlissIDs successfully registered!\n", voter_count);
    std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_secs(1));

    // Step 2: Create proposal
    println!("📋 STEP 2: Creating governance proposal...\n");
    
    let proposal = governance.proposal_manager.create_proposal(
        voters[0].0.clone(),
        "Upgrade AuraFS Protocol to v2.0".to_string(),
        "This proposal upgrades the network protocol to include quantum-safe routing".to_string(),
        ProposalType::NetworkUpgrade,
        48,
        Some({
            let mut meta = HashMap::new();
            meta.insert("version".to_string(), "2.0.0".to_string());
            meta
        }),
    )?;

    println!("  📄 Proposal ID: {}", proposal.id);
    println!("  📝 Title: {}", proposal.title);
    println!("  🕐 Voting Period: 48 hours");
    println!("  👤 Creator: {}\n", proposal.creator_bliss_id);
    
    std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_secs(1));

    // Step 3: Submit proposal for voting
    println!("🚀 STEP 3: Submitting proposal for voting...\n");
    
    let active_proposal = governance.proposal_manager.submit_proposal(&proposal.id)?;
    println!("  ✅ Proposal status: {:?}", active_proposal.status);
    println!("  📅 Voting started at: {}\n", 
        DateTime::<Local>::from(
            DateTime::from_timestamp(active_proposal.voting_start.unwrap(), 0).unwrap()
        ).format("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"));
    
    std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_secs(1));

    // Step 4: Cast votes
    println!("🗳️  STEP 4: Casting votes...\n");
    
    for (i, (bliss_id, sk)) in voters.iter().enumerate() {
        let option = if i < voter_count * 2 / 3 {
            VoteOption::Yes
        } else if i < voter_count * 5 / 6 {
            VoteOption::No
        } else {
            VoteOption::Abstain
        };

        let message = format!("{}:{:?}", proposal.id, option);
        let signature = dilithium5::detached_sign(message.as_bytes(), sk);

        let soul_proof = SoulProof {
            commitment: format!("proof_{}", i),
            proof_type: "zk-SNARK-unique-human".to_string(),
            verifiable: true,
        };

        let vote = governance.voting_engine.cast_vote(
            proposal.id.clone(),
            bliss_id.clone(),
            option.clone(),
            signature.as_bytes().to_vec(),
            soul_proof,
            None,
        ).await?;

        println!("  ✅ {} voted {:?} (weight: {:.2})", 
            bliss_id, vote.option, vote.vote_weight);
    }

    println!("\n📊 All votes cast!\n");
    std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_secs(1));

    // Step 5: Tally results
    println!("🔢 STEP 5: Tallying votes...\n");
    
    let tally = governance.voting_engine.tally_votes(&proposal.id);
    let approval_rate = (tally.yes / tally.total_weight) * 100.0;

    println!("  📈 Vote Tally:");
    println!("     YES:     {:.2} votes ({:.1}%)", tally.yes, (tally.yes / tally.total_weight) * 100.0);
    println!("     NO:      {:.2} votes ({:.1}%)", tally.no, (tally.no / tally.total_weight) * 100.0);
    println!("     ABSTAIN: {:.2} votes ({:.1}%)", tally.abstain, (tally.abstain / tally.total_weight) * 100.0);
    println!("     ───────────────────────────────");
    println!("     TOTAL:   {} souls, {:.2} weighted votes", tally.total_souls, tally.total_weight);
    println!("\n  💯 Approval Rate: {:.1}%\n", approval_rate);

    std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_secs(1));

    // Step 6: System diagnostics
    println!("🔍 STEP 6: System Diagnostics...\n");
    
    println!("  🏛️  Registered BlissIDs: {}", governance.blissid_manager.get_total_count());
    println!("  ✅ Active BlissIDs: {}", governance.blissid_manager.get_active_count());
    println!("  📋 Total Proposals: {}", governance.proposal_manager.list_proposals(None).len());
    println!("  🗳️  Total Votes Cast: {}", governance.voting_engine.list_votes(None).len());
    
    let integrity_valid = governance.verify_system_integrity()?;
    println!("  🔒 Audit Log Integrity: {}\n", if integrity_valid { "✅ VALID" } else { "❌ INVALID" });

    // Final summary
    println!("\n{}", "=".repeat(80));
    println!("🎉 DEMO COMPLETED SUCCESSFULLY!");
    println!("{}\n", "=".repeat(80));
    
    println!("Key Features Demonstrated:");
    println!("  ✅ One-Soul-Per-Account enforcement via BlissID");
    println!("  ✅ Quantum-safe signature verification (Dilithium5)");
    println!("  ✅ Soul-weighted voting with coherence scoring");
    println!("  ✅ Byzantine Fault Tolerant consensus");
    println!("  ✅ Cryptographic audit trail with integrity verification");
    println!("  ✅ Complete proposal lifecycle (Draft → Active → Finalized)");
    println!();

    Ok(())
}

// ==================== Interactive Commands ====================

async fn register_blissid_interactive(
    governance: Arc<GovernanceSystem>,
    did: String,
) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    println!("\n🆔 Registering new BlissID...\n");
    
    let soul_hash = format!("soul_{}", uuid::Uuid::new_v4());
    let soul_proof = SoulProof {
        commitment: format!("commit_{}", uuid::Uuid::new_v4()),
        proof_type: "zk-SNARK-unique-human".to_string(),
        verifiable: true,
    };

    let bliss_id = governance.blissid_manager.register_bliss_id(
        did.clone(),
        soul_hash,
        soul_proof,
    )?;

    println!("✅ BlissID registered successfully!");
    println!("   ID: {}", bliss_id.id);
    println!("   DID: {}", bliss_id.did);
    println!();

    Ok(())
}

async fn create_proposal_interactive(
    governance: Arc<GovernanceSystem>,
    creator: String,
    title: String,
    voting_period: i64,
) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    println!("\n📋 Creating new proposal...\n");
    
    let proposal = governance.proposal_manager.create_proposal(
        creator,
        title,
        "Interactive proposal creation".to_string(),
        ProposalType::ParameterChange,
        voting_period,
        None,
    )?;

    println!("✅ Proposal created successfully!");
    println!("   ID: {}", proposal.id);
    println!("   Status: {:?}", proposal.status);
    println!();

    Ok(())
}

async fn cast_vote_interactive(
    _governance: Arc<GovernanceSystem>,
    _proposal_id: String,
    _bliss_id: String,
    _option: String,
) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    println!("\n🗳️  Casting vote requires quantum signature...");
    println!("   Use the API or demo mode for complete voting workflow.\n");
    Ok(())
}

async fn show_diagnostics(
    governance: Arc<GovernanceSystem>,
) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    println!("\n🔍 SYSTEM DIAGNOSTICS\n");
    println!("═══════════════════════════════════════════════════\n");

    println!("📊 Identity System:");
    println!("   Total BlissIDs: {}", governance.blissid_manager.get_total_count());
    println!("   Active BlissIDs: {}", governance.blissid_manager.get_active_count());
    println!();

    println!("📋 Governance:");
    let proposals = governance.proposal_manager.list_proposals(None);
    println!("   Total Proposals: {}", proposals.len());
    println!("   Active: {}", proposals.iter().filter(|p| p.status == ProposalStatus::Active).count());
    println!("   Approved: {}", proposals.iter().filter(|p| p.status == ProposalStatus::Approved).count());
    println!();

    println!("🗳️  Voting:");
    let votes = governance.voting_engine.list_votes(None);
    println!("   Total Votes Cast: {}", votes.len());
    println!();

    println!("📜 Audit Log:");
    let entries = governance.audit_logger.get_entries();
    println!("   Total Events: {}", entries.len());
    println!();

    Ok(())
}

async fn verify_integrity(
    governance: Arc<GovernanceSystem>,
) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    println!("\n🔒 VERIFYING SYSTEM INTEGRITY\n");
    
    match governance.verify_system_integrity() {
        Ok(true) => {
            println!("✅ Audit log integrity: VALID");
            println!("   All cryptographic hashes verified.");
            println!("   Chain of custody intact.\n");
        }
        Ok(false) => {
            println!("❌ Audit log integrity: INVALID");
            println!("   System may have been tampered with.\n");
        }
        Err(e) => {
            println!("❌ Integrity check failed: {}\n", e);
        }
    }

    Ok(())
}

async fn export_audit_log(
    governance: Arc<GovernanceSystem>,
    output: String,
) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    println!("\n📤 Exporting audit log to {}...\n", output);
    
    let json = governance.audit_logger.export_json()?;
    std::fs::write(&output, json)?;

    println!("✅ Audit log exported successfully!\n");

    Ok(())
}

// ==================== Banner ====================

    println!("\n{}", "═".repeat(80));
    println!(r#"
     █████╗ ██╗   ██╗██████╗  █████╗ ███████╗███████╗
    ██╔══██╗██║   ██║██╔══██╗██╔══██╗██╔════╝██╔════╝
```

## **🚀 Usage Examples**

### **1. Run Interactive Demo**

```bash
cargo run --bin aurafs-governance -- demo --voters 10
```


### **2. Start API Server**

```bash
cargo run --bin aurafs-governance -- serve --port 8080
```


### **3. System Diagnostics**

```bash
cargo run --bin aurafs-governance -- diagnostics
```


### **4. Verify Integrity**

```bash
cargo run --bin aurafs-governance -- verify
```


### **5. Export Audit Log**

```bash
cargo run --bin aurafs-governance -- export --output audit.json
```


### **6. Register BlissID**

```bash
cargo run --bin aurafs-governance -- register-bliss-id --did "did:aurafs:alice"
```

## **📦 Complete `Cargo.toml`**

```toml
[package]
name = "afs-governance"
version = "1.0.0"
edition = "2021"
authors = ["Ross Edwards & Aurphyx"]
description = "AuraFS Governance System with BlissID and Soul-Weighted Voting"

[dependencies]
# Core
serde = { version = "1.0", features = ["derive"] }
serde_json = "1.0"
tokio = { version = "1.35", features = ["full"] }
uuid = { version = "1.6", features = ["v4", "serde"] }
chrono = { version = "0.4", features = ["serde"] }
log = "0.4"
env_logger = "0.11"

# Crypto
sha3 = "0.10"
hex = "0.4"
pqcrypto-dilithium = "0.5"
pqcrypto-traits = "0.3"

# Async
async-trait = "0.1"

# API
axum = "0.7"
tower = "0.4"
tower-http = { version = "0.5", features = ["cors"] }

# CLI
clap = { version = "4.5", features = ["derive"] }

[dev-dependencies]
tower = { version = "0.4", features = ["util"] }

[[bin]]
name = "aurafs-governance"
path = "src/main.rs"

[[bin]]
name = "aurafs-governance-server"
path = "src/server.rs"
```

# Is the 'afs/governance' the core files for the Global Voting System, Ineffable Ledger, and Opulence needs??

Excellent question! Let me clarify the **architectural relationship** between what we just built (`afs/governance`) and the **larger ecosystem components**. 📊

***

## **🏗️ Architecture Overview: Where Does `/afs/governance` Fit?**

### **Current Status: Foundation Layer ✅**

The `/afs/governance` module we just created is the **core governance engine** that serves as the **foundation** for:

1. ✅ **Global Voting System (Partially)**
2. ✅ **Ineffable Ledger Integration Points**
3. ⚠️ **Opulence/P4A (Needs Extension)**

***

## **📋 What We Have vs. What's Needed**

### **✅ What `/afs/governance` Currently Provides:**

| Component | Status | Coverage |
| :-- | :-- | :-- |
| **BlissID Registration \& Management** | ✅ Complete | One-soul-per-account enforcement |
| **Quantum Signature Verification** | ✅ Complete | Dilithium5 post-quantum crypto |
| **Soul-Weighted Voting** | ✅ Complete | Vote weight 1.0-1.5x based on coherence |
| **Proposal Lifecycle** | ✅ Complete | Draft → Active → Approved/Rejected → Executed |
| **BFT Consensus** | ✅ Complete | 2/3+ validator approval |
| **Audit Trail** | ✅ Complete | Cryptographic hash chain |
| **Policy Enforcement Hooks** | ✅ Complete | ACL, network, protocol updates |

### **⚠️ What Still Needs to Be Built:**

| Component | Status | What's Missing |
| :-- | :-- | :-- |
| **Global Voting System (Full)** | 🟡 Partial | - Frontend (web/mobile/CLI clients)<br>- ZK-Proof privacy layer<br>- Multi-voting modes (quadratic, ranked-choice)<br>- External federation API |
| **Ineffable Ledger (Core)** | ❌ Missing | - Merkle DAG blockchain implementation<br>- Archivus consensus (Tendermint-based BFT)<br>- Shard storage layer<br>- P2P network layer |
| **Opulence/P4A** | ❌ Missing | - Treasury management<br>- Token economics (Fux Coin)<br>- Wealth redistribution algorithms<br>- Quantum Market Oracle integration<br>- Prophetic P-Value simulation engine |

```
       └─────────────▶│ Ineffable │◀─────────────┘
                      │ (Ledger)  │
                      └───────────┘
```

**All repositories share:**

- ✅ Common `/afs/governance` core
- ✅ Unified data models
- ✅ Quantum-safe cryptography
- ✅ BFT consensus integration
- ✅ RESTful + WebSocket APIs

# Now let's create the diamond grade 'afs_sdk.rs', this is the python example script you gave for the AuraFS python version.                                                                                                  import os

import logging
import asyncio
from typing import Optional, List

from namespace.namespace_manager import NamespaceManager
from snapshot.snapshot_manager import SnapshotManager
from encryption.crypto_manager import QSigner
from deduplication.dedupe_engine import DeduplicationEngine
from afs.acl.aclmanager import ACLManager
from cache_prefetch.cache_manager import CachePrefetchManager

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format='%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s')

class AuraFSClientSDK:
    def __init__(self,
                 shard_server_url: str = "http://localhost:5000",
                 cache_capacity: int = 1000):
        self.ns_manager = NamespaceManager()
        self.snapshot_manager = SnapshotManager()
        self.crypto_manager = QSigner()
        self.dedupe_engine = DeduplicationEngine()
        self.acl_manager = ACLManager()
        self.cache_manager = CachePrefetchManager(cache_capacity)
        self.shard_server_url = shard_server_url

def create_file(self, path: str, content: bytes, user_id: str) -> bool:
        if not self.acl_manager.is_allowed(user_id, path, "write"):
            logging.warning(f"User {user_id} permission denied to write {path}")
            return False

temp_file_path = f"/tmp/aura_tmp_{os.urandom(4).hex()}"
        with open(temp_file_path, "wb") as f:
            f.write(content)
       
        chunk_hashes = self.dedupe_engine.add_file(temp_file_path)
        os.remove(temp_file_path)

file_metadata = {
            "size": len(content),
            "owner": user_id,
            "chunk_hashes": chunk_hashes,
        }

result = self.ns_manager.create_file(path, metadata=file_metadata)
        logging.info(f"File {path} created with result: {result}")
        return result

def read_file(self, path: str, user_id: str) -> Optional[bytes]:
        if not self.acl_manager.is_allowed(user_id, path, "read"):
            logging.warning(f"User {user_id} permission denied to read {path}")
            return None

meta = self.ns_manager.get_metadata(path)
        if not meta or meta.get("type") != "file":
            logging.error(f"File {path} not found")
            return None

chunk_hashes = meta.get("chunk_hashes", [])
        data_parts = []
        for ch in chunk_hashes:
            cached = self.cache_manager.lookup(ch)
            if cached:
                data_parts.append(cached)
                continue
            chunk_data = asyncio.run(self._fetch_chunk_remote(ch))
            if chunk_data:
                self.cache_manager.store(ch, chunk_data)
                data_parts.append(chunk_data)
            else:
                logging.error(f"Missing chunk {ch} in shard server")
                return None
        return b"".join(data_parts)

async def _fetch_chunk_remote(self, chunk_hash: str) -> Optional[bytes]:
        \# Placeholder: Fetch chunk from shard server with retry, auth, etc.
        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
        logging.info(f"Fetched chunk {chunk_hash} from remote store")
        return None  \# simulate fetch failure for demo

def create_snapshot(self, description: str) -> str:
        backup_dir = self.ns_manager.data_dir
        snap_id = self.snapshot_manager.create_snapshot(backup_dir, description)
        logging.info(f"Snapshot created: {snap_id}")
        return snap_id

def rollback_to_snapshot(self, snapshot_id: str) -> bool:
        try:
            self.snapshot_manager.rollback(snapshot_id)
            logging.info(f"Rollback to snapshot {snapshot_id} succeeded")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            logging.error(f"Rollback failed: {e}")
            return False

def sign_data(self, data: bytes) -> bytes:
        return self.crypto_manager.sign(data)

def verify_signature(self, data: bytes, signature: bytes) -> bool:
        return self.crypto_manager.verify(data, signature)

def shutdown(self):
        self.cache_manager.shutdown()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    sdk = AuraFSClientSDK()
    user = "alice"
    test_path = "/docs/hello.txt"
    test_content = b"Hello AuraFS!"

if sdk.create_file(test_path, test_content, user):
        content = sdk.read_file(test_path, user)
        print(f"Read file content: {content}")

snap_id = sdk.create_snapshot("Initial backup")
    print(f"Snapshot created: {snap_id}")

sig = sdk.sign_data(test_content)
    print(f"Signature valid: {sdk.verify_signature(test_content, sig)}")

sdk.shutdown()
